Under this stronger evaluation, the apparent robustness gap between expensive defenses and PoE narrows considerably, while PoE remains substantially cheaper and preserves higher-quality reasoning traces. Overall, our results suggest that strong distillation remains difficult to stop, and that progress on antidistillation should be judged against adaptive students rather than passive ones. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ysfalh/distillation-game.

</details>


### [224] [Lost in Tokenization: Fundamental Trade-offs in Graph Tokenization for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22471)
*Maya Bechler-Speicher,Gilad Yehudai,Gil Harari,Clayton Sanford,Amir Globerson,Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实验两个角度证明：图Transformer的分词方式（tokenization）是模型表达能力的关键因素，不同分词方式会导致不同的深度复杂度和表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络研究中，Transformer需要将图结构转化为序列输入，但分词方式的选择对模型能力有何影响尚不明确。作者旨在揭示分词选择与Transformer表达能力之间的根本联系，为图学习模型设计提供理论指导。

Method: 1. 理论分析三种基础分词方式：谱分词、随机游走分词、邻接分词
2. 建立深度复杂度理论框架，证明不同分词方式所需的Transformer深度差异
3. 给出不可行性结果和下界证明
4. 通过合成任务和真实任务的受控实验验证理论预测

Result: 1. 随机游走分词无论游走长度如何都是有损的，无法从分词结果恢复原图
2. 谱分词是无损的，但在局部任务上条件数差
3. 有限深度Transformer无法在不同分词家族间进行转换
4. 不同任务偏好不同的结构视角，组合互补的分词方式可以让Transformer利用多种信号

Conclusion: 分词方式的选择是图Transformer表达能力的根本组成部分。不当的分词方式可能阻碍模型有效恢复合适的结构表示。实际应用中应根据具体任务选择匹配的分词方式，或采用组合策略融合不同分词的互补信息。

Abstract: Transformers have become a central architecture for graph learning, but their application to graphs requires first choosing a tokenization: a graph-to-token map that determines which structural information is exposed at the input. In this work, we show that this choice is a fundamental component of transformer expressivity. We examine three tokenizations that serve as building blocks for many existing graph tokenizations: spectral, random-walk, and adjacency tokenizations. We prove that different tokenizations induce distinct depth regimes: the same graph computation may be realizable by a shallow transformer under one tokenization, while requiring substantially larger depth under another. For example, we prove that random-walk tokenization is lossy for any walk length, making it impossible in general to recover the graph from it, and that while spectral tokenization is lossless, it is ill-conditioned for local tasks. We further show that although both random-walk and spectral tokenizations are derived from adjacency information, it is impossible for a limited-depth transformer to convert between tokenization families in general. In particular, we establish lower bounds and impossibility results showing that unfavorable tokenizations may preclude the efficient recovery of more suitable structural representations. Finally, we complement our theory with controlled experiments on synthetic and real-world tasks, validating the predicted separations and showing that different tasks favor different structural views, and combining complementary tokenizations allows the transformer to leverage distinct signals from each representation.

</details>


### [225] [Proxy-Based Approximation of Shapley and Banzhaf Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22738)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Hubert Baniecki,R. Teal Witter,Eyke Hüllermeier,Maximilian Muschalik,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProxySHAP，一种用于估计Shapley和Banzhaf交互的新方法，通过结合树代理模型的高样本效率和残差校正的一致性，在多项式时间内计算树集成的精确交互指数，显著优于现有最佳估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Shapley和Banzhaf交互估计器在速度和准确性之间存在权衡，无法同时实现高样本效率和理论一致性，且先前方法在树深度上存在指数依赖问题。

Method: 提出了ProxySHAP方法，将树代理模型的高样本效率与残差校正相结合实现一致性；理论上推导了干预型TreeSHAP的多项式时间泛化，用于计算树集成的精确交互指数；形式化分析了残差调整策略，刻画了最大样本重用（MSR）在特定条件下校正代理偏差而不产生指数级方差的条件。

Result: 在多项式时间内成功计算树集成的精确交互指数，避免了指数级树深度依赖；残差调整策略在特定条件下可校正代理偏差且方差不随交互规模呈指数增长；大量基准测试表明ProxySHAP在小预算和大预算情况下均达到最低误差，显著优于ProxySPEX和KernelSHAP-IQ等先前最佳估计器，在包含数千特征的大规模应用中设定了新的最先进标准。

Conclusion: ProxySHAP成功调和了速度与准确性的权衡，为高阶交互估计设定了新的最先进标准，在近似质量和下游可解释性任务中均表现出卓越性能，为大规模机器学习应用中的复杂动态分析提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Shapley and Banzhaf interactions capture the complex dynamics inherent in modern machine learning applications. However, current estimators for these higher-order interactions trade off between speed and accuracy. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProxySHAP. ProxySHAP reconciles the high sample efficiency of tree-based proxy models with a principled path to consistency via residual correction. On a theoretical level, we derive a polynomial-time generalization of interventional TreeSHAP to compute exact interaction indices for tree ensembles, successfully bypassing exponential tree-depth dependencies in prior methods. Furthermore, we formally analyze the residual adjustment strategy, characterizing the specific conditions under which Maximum Sample Reuse (MSR) corrects proxy bias without its variance scaling exponentially with interaction size. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that ProxySHAP sets a new state-of-the-art standard for approximation quality, including in large-scale applications with thousands of features. By achieving the lowest error in both small- and large-budget regimes, ProxySHAP significantly outperforms the prior best estimators ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ, while also delivering superior performance on downstream explainability tasks.

</details>


### [226] [Winner-Take-All bottlenecks enforce disentangled symbolic representations in multi-task learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22472)
*Julian Gutheil,Simon Hitzginger,Robert Legenstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在深度神经网络中加入胜者通吃（WTA）瓶颈，可在多任务学习设置下强制提取类别性潜在因子，并在理论证明与实验验证下展示了所获得的表示具有高度符号化特性，从而提升泛化能力并可作为符号与亚符号AI之间的接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WTA网络是大脑皮层网络的核心电路模体，类似机制（如Transformer注意力层的softmax）在深度学习中广泛存在。然而，其在高度非线性纠缠的潜在因子中的作用尚不明确。已有研究仅关注简单生成模型中WTA的作用，缺乏对其在复杂非线性场景下表征能力与泛化优势的系统理解。

Method: 在深度神经网络中引入WTA瓶颈，并在多任务学习框架下进行理论分析。研究者首先在特定定义良好的条件下证明：WTA瓶颈能够强制网络提取类别性潜在因子；随后展示该瓶颈处产生的表示具有高度符号化特性——单个神经元或神经元群体编码单一抽象特征（如物体、颜色或位置）。此外，在两个数据集上对不完全符合定理假设的架构和设置进行实验验证。

Result: 理论证明表明，在满足定义良好条件时，WTA瓶颈可确保类别性潜在因子的提取；瓶颈处学得的表示高度符号化，单个神经元或神经元群对应单一抽象特征。实验表明，即使在架构不完全满足定理假设的情况下，该结论仍近似成立，且获得的符号化表示能带来更好的泛化性能。所提模型为理解含WTA类组件的深度神经网络的泛化能力提供了新视角。

Conclusion: WTA瓶颈在深度网络中可作为一种机制，强制产生符号化、可解释的表示，从而促进多任务学习下的潜在因子提取与泛化性能提升。该模型为弥合符号AI与亚符号AI系统之间的鸿沟提供了潜在接口，深化了对WTA机制在生物与人工神经网络中功能的理解。

Abstract: Winner-take-all (WTA) networks constitute a central circuit motif in cortical networks of the brain. In addition, WTA-like activations are abundant in modern deep learning models in the form of the softmax activation for example in attention layers of transformers. While their role in the extraction of latent factors has been studied for relatively simple generative models, their role in the context of highly non-linearly entangled latent factors has remained elusive. In this article, we show that a WTA bottleneck within a deep neural network can enforce under certain well-defined conditions the extraction of categorical latent factors of the data in a multi-task learning setup. In particular, we prove that the representation that emerges in the WTA bottleneck is highly symbolic, where a single neuron or a population of neurons encodes the presence of a single abstract feature such as a specific object, color, or position. We furthermore show empirically on two datasets, that this also holds for architectures and setups that do not fully comply with the assumptions of our theorem and demonstrate the advantages of the acquired symbolic representation for generalization. Our proposed model provides insights into the generalization capabilities of deep neural networks with WTA-like components and may serve as an interface between symbolic and subsymbolic AI systems.

</details>


### [227] [Cyber-Physical Anomaly Detection in IoT-Enabled Smart Grids Using Machine Learning and Metaheuristic Feature Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22749)
*Adis Alihodžić,Eva Tuba,Milan Tuba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合机器学习和遗传算法特征选择的方法，用于智能电网中网络攻击与自然事件的分类，成功将PMU特征从112个降至27.4个，同时提升了分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能电网依赖密集的测量基础设施和智能设备，虽然提高了监控和控制能力，但也增加了网络物理干扰的脆弱性。操作人员需要区分物理事件（如故障、线路干扰）与恶意行为（如虚假数据注入、未授权命令执行），这对电网安全运行至关重要。

Method: 使用MSU/ORNL电力系统攻击数据集，采用遗传算法（GA）进行特征选择，结合多种机器学习模型进行评估，包括逻辑回归、RBF-SVM、XGBoost、随机森林和Extra Trees等基线模型。

Result: 树形集成模型表现最佳，其中Extra Trees为最强基线。GA+Extra Trees模型将PMU特征空间从112个属性降至平均27.4个属性，宏F1从0.9118提升至0.9212，ROC-AUC从0.9791提升至0.9837。

Conclusion: 许多同步电气测量存在冗余性，通过遗传算法选择的一个紧凑的相量特征子集仍然能够在智能电网中提供准确且可解释的异常检测，为实际应用提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern smart grids rely on dense measurement infrastructures, communication links, and intelligent field devices. Although this improves supervision and control, it also increases vulnerability to cyber-physical disruptions. Operators must distinguish physical incidents, such as faults or line disturbances, from malicious actions, such as false data injection or unauthorized command execution. This chapter investigates this problem using the well-known MSU/ORNL Power System Attack Dataset. The proposed method combines machine learning with genetic-algorithm-based feature selection. The objective is twofold: to classify attack and natural events accurately, and to determine whether a reduced set of physically informative PMU/IED measurements can support reliable detection. Several baseline models are evaluated, including logistic regression, RBF-SVM, XGBoost, Random Forest, and Extra Trees. The results show that tree-based ensemble models are the most effective for the considered dataset, with Extra Trees providing the strongest full-feature baseline. After feature selection, the GA + Extra Trees model reduces the clean PMU feature space from 112 attributes to an average of 27.4 attributes over five runs, while increasing macro-F1 from 0.9118 to 0.9212 and ROC-AUC from 0.9791 to 0.9837. These results indicate that many synchronized electrical measurements are redundant. A compact subset of phasor-based features can still provide accurate and interpretable anomaly detection in smart grids.

</details>


### [228] [Structured-Sparse Attention for Entity Tracking with Subquadratic Sequence Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22476)
*Hangyue Zhao,Paul Caillon,Erwan Fagnou,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种次二次复杂度的分块注意力计算方法，在保持实体跟踪任务准确率的同时，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降低至O(n^{4/3}d)，实现了12-29%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体跟踪任务需要在长序列中维护和更新实体的潜在状态，现有任务特定的注意力算子虽然能将深层Transformer压缩为几层，但其密集评估计算代价仍然昂贵。作者观察到学习到的注意力具有强结构特性：大部分质量集中在局部块对角邻域内，仅有少量跨块残留，这为优化提供了机会。

Method: 提出了一种预解式算子的分块评估方法：将块内交互保持精确计算，将跨块交互通过一个缩减系统进行路由。该方法利用了注意力分布的块对角结构特性，避免了全序列的二次方复杂度计算。

Result: 计算复杂度降至O(n^{4/3}d)（当d≈n时为O(n^{7/3})）；在受控跟踪基准测试中，准确率与密集算子相当，墙钟时间减少12-29%；在相近精确匹配准确率下，比紧凑密集Transformer快达2.4倍。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了实体跟踪任务的加速，但存在局限性：当同时演化的属性数量超过注意力头数量时，性能会崩塌。消融实验分析了块大小和模型容量的影响，为实际应用提供了指导。

Abstract: Entity tracking requires maintaining and updating latent states for entities and attributes over long sequences. Recent task-specific attention operators can compress deep Transformer stacks into a few layers by performing multi-hop state propagation within a single layer, but their dense evaluation remains expensive. We show that in this setting, learned attention is strongly structured: most mass concentrates in local block-diagonal neighborhoods with a light cross-block residue. Exploiting this, we derive a blockwise evaluation of a resolvent-style operator that keeps within-block interactions exact and routes cross-block interactions through a reduced system. The resulting evaluation is subquadratic in sequence length $O(n^{4/3}d)$ (and $O(n^{7/3})$ when $d\approx n$). On controlled tracking benchmarks, our method matches the dense operator's accuracy while reducing wall-clock time by $12-29\%$ under a standardized measurement protocol, and is up to $2.4 \times$ faster than a compact dense Transformer at comparable exact-match accuracy. We further provide ablations over block size and model capacity, and identify a limitation: performance collapses when the number of simultaneously evolving properties exceeds the number of attention heads.

</details>


### [229] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CogAdapt框架，通过LeadBridge适配器和ProFine渐进微调策略，成功将临床ECG基础模型迁移至可穿戴设备的认知负荷评估任务，在跨被试验证中显著优于从头训练的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时认知负荷评估对自适应人机交互至关重要，但面临标注数据有限和跨被试泛化能力差的挑战。虽然已有在百万临床记录上预训练的ECG基础模型，但由于传感器配置不匹配和任务差异，无法直接应用于可穿戴设备。

Method: 提出CogAdapt框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) LeadBridge可学习适配器，将3导联可穿戴信号转换为解剖学一致的12导联表示；(2) ProFine渐进微调策略，逐步解冻编码器层同时防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在两个公开数据集（CLARE和CL-Drive）上采用留一被试验证，CogAdapt的macro-F1分数分别达到0.626和0.768，显著优于从头训练的基线模型。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了基础模型自适应方法在可穿戴传感器独立于被试的认知负荷评估任务中的有效性，为解决跨领域迁移问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [230] [MambaGaze: Bidirectional Mamba with Explicit Missing Data Modeling for Cognitive Load Assessment from Eye-Gaze Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22775)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MambaGaze框架，通过XMD编码和双向Mamba-2解决眼动信号实时认知负荷评估中的数据缺失和长程依赖建模问题，在准确率和实时性上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时认知负荷评估对于驾驶员警觉性监控、飞行甲板辅助等安全关键型人机交互AI应用具有重要价值，但现有方法面临两大挑战：1) 眨眼和跟踪失败导致的频繁数据缺失；2) 长程时间依赖关系的高效建模。

Method: 提出MambaGaze框架，包含两个核心组件：1) XMD编码，通过融合观测掩码和时间增量信息来显式建模数据不确定性；2) 双向Mamba-2架构，以线性计算复杂度捕获时间依赖关系。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集的留一受试者交叉验证中，MambaGaze分别达到76.8%和73.1%的准确率，较CNN、Transformer、ResNet和VGG基线模型提升4-12个百分点。在NVIDIA Jetson平台的边缘部署测试中实现43-68 FPS的实时推理，功耗低于7.5W。

Conclusion: MambaGaze成功解决了眼动信号认知负荷评估中的数据缺失和计算效率问题，在保持高准确率的同时实现了低功耗实时推理，验证了可穿戴认知负荷监测的可行性。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment from eye-tracking signals could potentially enable adaptive human-centered-AI such as safety-critical applications such as driver vigilance monitoring or automated flight deck assistance, yet two challenges persist: handling frequent data missingness from blinks and tracking failures, and efficiently modeling long-range temporal dependencies. We propose MambaGaze, a framework that addresses these challenges through 1) XMD encoding, which augments raw features with observation masks and time-deltas to explicitly model data uncertainty, and 2) bidirectional Mamba-2, which captures temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity. Experiments on CLARE and CL-Drive datasets under leave-one-subject-out evaluation show that MambaGaze achieves 76.8% and 73.1% accuracy, respectively, outperforming CNN, Transformer, ResNet, and VGG baselines by 4-12 percentage points. Edge deployment benchmarks on NVIDIA Jetson platforms demonstrate real-time inference at 43-68 FPS with power consumption below 7.5W, confirming feasibility for wearable cognitive load monitoring.

</details>


### [231] [When Stronger Triggers Backfire: A High-Dimensional Theory of Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22481)
*Donald Flynn,Hadas Yaron Goldhirsh,Jonathan P. Keating,Inbar Seroussi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在GLM模型中发现一种反直觉现象：增强训练触发器强度反而有助于防御者，揭示了高维后门攻击中存在一个最优触发器强度临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维后门中毒攻击中一个反直觉现象：增强训练触发器强度可能反而帮助防御者，并揭示其背后的理论机制。

Method: 采用正则化广义线性模型，在比例极限 regime (p/n → κ) 下研究高斯混合数据，通过闭式解(平方损失)和高斯代理不动点系统(一般凸GLM损失)进行理论分析，并在CIFAR-10和ResNet-18上验证。

Result: 发现三个关键现象：(i) 干净测试精度随触发器强度α增加而提高；(ii) 攻击成功率在有限α处达峰后下降；(iii) 最危险的触发器方向是数据协方差的最小特征向量。识别出与κ成比例的有限样本噪声底是现象(i)的机制，这在经典n≫p分析中不可见。

Conclusion: 证明了高维后门攻击中存在最优触发器强度的临界点，超越此临界点后增强触发器反而降低攻击效果，为防御策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Backdoor poisoning attacks behave counter-intuitively in high dimensions: stronger training triggers can help the defender. We study regularised generalised linear models on Gaussian-mixture data in the proportional regime ($p/n \to κ$), varying the training trigger strength $α$ against a fixed test trigger. Three phenomena emerge: (i) clean test accuracy increases with $α$; (ii) attack success peaks at a finite $α$ and then declines; and (iii) the most damaging trigger direction is the minimum eigenvector of the data covariance. We prove all three results in closed form for the squared loss, and extend (i) and (ii) to general convex GLM losses via a Gaussian-proxy fixed-point system. We identify a finite-sample noise floor proportional to $κ$ as the mechanism behind (i), invisible to classical $n \gg p$ analysis. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and Gaussian surrogates match the theory closely; ResNet-18 experiments show the same phenomena beyond the convex setting.

</details>


### [232] [SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22776)
*Stanislav R. Kirpichenko,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Lev V. Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了生存扩散概率模型（SDPM），一种基于去噪扩散模型的连续时间生存分析方法，避免了传统方法对风险函数的结构假设或时间轴离散化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存分析方法通常对风险函数施加结构假设或对时间轴进行离散化，这些做法限制了模型的灵活性并引入近似误差，因此需要一种能够直接建模连续时间生存分布的灵活方法。

Method: 提出生存扩散概率模型（SDPM），使用去噪扩散模型建模生存结果的联合条件分布P(T,δ|x)，其中T为观测时间，δ为删失指示符。在条件独立删失假设下，通过Kaplan-Meier估计器将条件样本转换为生存函数估计。模型在变换后的目标空间中操作，采用标准化对数时间和删失指示符的连续高斯混合表示，避免了对事件时间分布的参数假设和输出时间空间的离散化。

Result: 在十个真实生存数据集上的实验表明，SDPM在C-index、时间依赖性AUC和Brier评分上均达到竞争性表现。合成Cox-Weibull数据研究表明，当生成足够多样本时，SDPM能比强非参数基线更准确地恢复潜在连续生存分布的形状。消融研究证实目标空间变换的重要性，改善了事件率校准、减少无效生成时间，并在预测判别上提供一致增益。

Conclusion: SDPM成功将扩散生成模型应用于生存分析任务，提供了一种灵活的连续时间生存分析方法，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时避免了传统方法的局限性，为生存分析领域提供了新的建模思路。

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.

</details>


### [233] [Represented Is Not Computed: A Causal Test of Candidate Algorithmic Intermediates in a Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22488)
*Ishita Darade,Sushrut Thorat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过算术任务发现，线性探针可以解码出Transformer的算法中间变量，但因果分析显示模型实际使用的计算路径与探针暗示的路径不同，揭示了探针方法与因果观察之间可能存在显著分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言和视觉任务中，结构化提示的组件整合机制难以精确分析，因为任务相关关系缺乏精确定义。作者选择算术任务（基数-数字提取）作为更清晰的研究场景，因为该任务有明确的闭式解和可验证的算法中间步骤，便于对模型的内部算法进行精确分析。

Method: 1. 训练Transformer模型执行基数-数字提取任务：给定N、B和D，输出N在B进制展开中B^D的系数（闭式解为⌊N/B^D⌋ mod B）。
2. 使用线性探针解码模型的算法中间变量。
3. 进行因果测试，分离表示与使用，分析D输入流到输出位置的局部路径。
4. 执行稀疏电路搜索，识别N、B、D的处理路径及其组合方式。

Result: 1. 模型在保留数字-基数组合上达到99.83%准确率，建立了可靠的任务能力。
2. 线性探针成功解码算法中间变量，使分阶段算术计算成为可能。
3. 因果测试发现早期D选择性通信与N、B无关。
4. 稀疏电路搜索发现N、B、D主要通过分离路径处理，在后期而非探针建议的分阶段路径中组合。

Conclusion: 模型确实表示了闭式解的中间步骤，但识别出的局部因果路径并未将这些中间变量传递到输出流。这个案例表明，即使存在明确的算法假设，基于探针的结论也可能与因果观察结果产生显著分歧，为神经网络可解释性研究提供了重要警示。

Abstract: Structured prompts require integrating components according to task-relevant relations. How a network implements this integration is often hard to judge in language or vision, where those relations are rarely specified precisely enough to define a candidate internal algorithm. Arithmetic offers a cleaner setting. We study a Transformer trained on base-digit extraction: given $N$, $B$, and $D$, it must report the coefficient of $B^D$ in the base-$B$ expansion of $N$. The closed-form solution, $\lfloor N/B^D \rfloor \bmod B$, provides explicit candidate algorithmic intermediates. Across three seeds, the model reaches 99.83% exact-answer accuracy on held-out number-base intersections, establishing reliable task competence. Linear probes decode the intermediates, making staged arithmetic computation plausible. Causal tests then separate representation from use: within the localized route from the stream with $D$ as input to the output positions, behavior depends on early $D$-selective communication, independent of $N$ and $B$. Relatedly, a sparse circuit search finds mostly separate $N$, $B$, and $D$ routes that combine late rather than the staged route suggested by the probes. Thus, the model represents the intermediates that make the closed-form solution plausible, but the identified localized causal route does not transmit them to the output stream. This case shows that probe-based conclusions can diverge sharply from causal observations, even when explicit algorithmic hypotheses are available.

</details>


### [234] [The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22800)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“匹配原则”统一框架，将鲁棒性、域适应、遮挡不变性等七个看似独立的问题归结为同一个统计问题：估计标签保持的部署扰动协方差，并在覆盖该协方差的矩阵范围内对编码器雅可比矩阵进行正则化。在线性-高斯模型中证明了闭式最优性，并引入轨迹偏离指数（TDI）作为嵌入敏感性的无标签探测方法。通过13个预注册实验验证了匹配、各向同性、错误顺序的预测，其中12个通过验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将鲁棒性、域适应、光度与遮挡不变性、组合泛化、时间鲁棒性、对齐安全以及经典各向异性正则化等问题视为独立问题处理，产生了各自独立的方法家族。本文认为这些问题的共享结构本质上是同一个统计问题，试图建立统一的理论框架来解释和连接这些看似不同的方法。

Method: 提出匹配原则：估计标签保持的部署扰动协方差，然后在覆盖该协方差的矩阵范围内对编码器雅可比矩阵进行正则化。证明现有方法（CORAL、对抗训练、IRM、数据增强、度量学习、雅可比惩罚、对齐约束）都是该对象的不同估计器而非独立的鲁棒性技巧。在线性-高斯模型中推导闭式解，包括匹配范围内的立方根注水策略。引入轨迹偏离指数（TDI）作为无标签嵌入敏感性探测方法，当任务准确率或雅可比Frobenius范数不足时使用。

Result: 在线性-高斯模型中证明了闭式最优性（定理A），包括匹配范围内的立方根注水；证明了二次雅可比惩罚范围覆盖的必要性（定理G）；验证了深度全局最小值处的相同范围二分法；提出两个证伪控制（引理C；推论E）和七个条件一致性引理（D1-D7）。在13个预注册实验中，从经典ML到Qwen2.5-7B规模，验证了预测的匹配→各向同性→错误W顺序在几何和部署漂移上的表现，其中12个通过验证，唯一例外（Office-31）是由于事先命名特征值间隙失败。在7B规模上，匹配式PMH提高选择性诚实度并保持风格TDI，而标准DPO则使其退化。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于命名部署扰动协方差这一关键对象，阐明正则化器必须执行的操作，并在识别该对象后提供闭式的可证伪理论。这并非追求在每个排行榜上的普适性，而是提供一个统一的理论框架，将多种鲁棒性方法重新理解为同一统计问题的不同估计器。该工作为理解和设计鲁棒性方法提供了新的理论基础和可检验的预测。

Abstract: Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks.
  In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions.
  We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient.
  Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it.
  The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.

</details>


### [235] [The Signal in the Noise: OOD Detection Through Goodness-of-Fit Testing in Factorised Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22496)
*Philipp Bomatter,Jack Geary,Henry Gouk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续归一化流的OOD检测新方法SITN，通过利用流模型的微分同胚和质量守恒特性，解决了传统似然方法在分布外检测中不可靠的问题，无需OOD数据且计算开销小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型虽然为分布外检测提供了自然基础，但先前研究表明其似然值在区分分布内外数据时极不可靠。作者旨在解决这一根本问题，利用连续归一化流的特殊性质开发更有效的检测方法。

Method: 利用连续归一化流的微分同胚和质量守恒特性，分析发现OOD样本被映射为噪声先验下高度非典型的噪声样本，这种异常无法被似然值捕获。基于此提出Signal in the Noise(SITN)方法，在单样本级别进行OOD检测，无需OOD数据访问，计算开销极小。

Result: 该方法能够提供严格的假阳性率控制，通过标准基准和合成扰动的全面评估，证明了方法的有效性，且避免了基于似然方法固有的复杂性偏差问题。

Conclusion: SITN方法成功克服了传统似然方法的局限性，为分布外检测提供了一种可靠、高效且可解释的解决方案，具有严格的统计保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Deep generative models offer a natural foundation for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, yet prior work has shown that their assigned likelihoods are notoriously unreliable indicators for in- vs out-of-distribution data. In this paper, we address this problem by leveraging the diffeomorphic and mass-preserving properties of continuous normalising flows. Our analysis shows that OOD samples are mapped to noise samples that are highly atypical under the noise prior in ways not captured by the likelihood. Based on this observation, we propose a new method -- Signal in the Noise (SITN) -- for OOD detection on the single-sample level. SITN requires no access to OOD data, incurs minimal computational overhead, and provides strict control of false positive rates. Comprehensive evaluations through standard benchmarks and synthetic perturbations highlight the method's effectiveness and the absence of the complexity bias inherent to likelihood-based methods.

</details>


### [236] [Vector Policy Optimization: Training for Diversity Improves Test-Time Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22817)
*Ryan Bahlous-Boldi,Isha Puri,Idan Shenfeld,Akarsh Kumar,Mehul Damani,Sebastian Risi,Omar Khattab,Zhang-Wei Hong,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出向量策略优化（VPO）算法，通过优化向量奖励来训练LLM生成多样化解决方案，显著提升了测试时搜索性能，特别是在进化搜索中能解决GRPO无法解决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练范式优化预定义的标量奖励，导致模型产生低熵响应分布，缺乏推理时搜索所需的多样性。随着语言模型需要在推理扩展搜索程序（如AlphaEvolve）中泛化到新环境并处理多种任务特定奖励函数，这种多样性不足成为关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出向量策略优化（VPO）算法，利用实践中奖励通常是向量值的特点（如代码生成中每个测试用例的正确性、不同用户角色或奖励模型），将GRPO优势估计器替换为能够训练LLM输出解决方案集合的方法，其中各个解专门针对向量奖励空间中的不同权衡。VPO显式训练策略以预期多样化的下游奖励函数并产生多样化解决方案。

Result: 在四个任务上，VPO在测试时搜索指标（如pass@k和best@k）上匹配或超越最强标量RL基线，且随着搜索预算增加，性能差距扩大。在进化搜索中，VPO模型能够解锁GRPO模型完全无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 随着测试时搜索变得更加标准化，优化多样性可能需要成为默认的后训练目标。VPO为LLM在需要多样化输出的推理时搜索场景中提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Language models must now generalize out of the box to novel environments and work inside inference-scaling search procedures, such as AlphaEvolve, that select rollouts with a variety of task-specific reward functions. Unfortunately, the standard paradigm of LLM post-training optimizes a pre-specified scalar reward, often leading current LLMs to produce low-entropy response distributions and thus to struggle at displaying the diversity that inference-time search will require. We propose Vector Policy Optimization (VPO), an RL algorithm that explicitly trains policies to anticipate diverse downstream reward functions and to produce diverse solutions. VPO exploits that rewards are often vector-valued in practice, like per-test-case correctness in code generation or, say, multiple different user personas or reward models. VPO is essentially a drop-in replacement for the GRPO advantage estimator, but it trains the LLM to output a set of solutions where individual solutions specialize to different trade-offs in the vector reward space. Across four tasks, VPO matches or beats the strongest scalar RL baselines on test-time search (e.g. pass@k and best@k), with the gap widening as the search budget grows. For evolutionary search, VPO models unlock problems that GRPO models cannot solve at all. As test-time search becomes more standardized, optimizing for diversity may need to become the default post-training objective.

</details>


### [237] [Generative Modeling by Value-Driven Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22507)
*Pablo Moreno-Muñoz,Adrian Müller,Gergely Neu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间随机控制的新型生成建模框架，通过线性规划对偶变量学习最优值函数，实现了高效的无模拟训练，生成质量优异且路径更直。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流模型、扩散模型和薛定谔桥方法在生成建模中存在路径不够直、模拟计算成本高、采样速度慢等问题。作者受控制理论启发，希望构建一个既能学习最优传输策略，又具有高效训练和推理能力的生成模型框架。

Method: 将测度传输问题转化为离散时间随机控制问题，并表述为线性规划形式。其对偶变量对应控制问题的最优值函数，可直接编码最优控制策略。基于此LP形式，提出无需模拟的原始-对偶算法，学习近似最优值函数和对应的价值驱动传输（VDT）策略。

Result: 训练良好的VDT策略具有直的传输路径，模拟快速且鲁棒；在条件生成、无分类器引导、非配对数据翻译等增强功能上易于实现。实验结果表明该方法性能强劲，扩展潜力良好。

Conclusion: 所提VDT框架成功将控制理论与生成建模结合，通过LP对偶学习最优值函数，避免了传统扩散模型的低效模拟，生成了更直的路径，展示了优于现有流、扩散和薛定谔桥方法的潜力和扩展性。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for generative modeling based on a discrete-time stochastic control formulation of measure transport. Adapting classic results from control theory, we formulate our problem as a linear program whose dual variables correspond to the \emph{optimal value function} of the control problem, which directly encodes the optimal control policy. Exploiting this LP formulation, we develop an efficient simulation-free primal-dual algorithm for computing approximately optimal value functions and the associated \emph{value-driven transport} (VDT) policies which approximate the true optimal policy. We show that well-trained VDT policies enjoy numerous favorable properties in comparison with other state-of-the-art methods based on flows, diffusions, or Schrödinger bridges: they lead to straight transport paths which can be simulated quickly and robustly, and can be enhanced in all the same ways as diffusion and flow-based models (e.g., conditional generation, classifier-free guidance, unpaired data-to-data translation are all easy to incorporate). We evaluate our methodology in a range of experiments, with results that indicate strong performance and good potential for scalability.

</details>


### [238] [Disentanglement Beyond Generative Models with Riemannian ICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22531)
*Edmond Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了黎曼ICA（RICA）理论，通过局部几何结构替代全局生成模型假设，引入解纠缠张量实现点态解纠缠，为现代表示学习中的解纠缠现象提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解纠缠理论框架（如ICA及其非线性变体）基于强生成假设，难以应用于现代表示学习；而现代预训练编码器虽能学习具有解纠缠特性的特征，却缺乏不依赖生成假设的一般性理论解释，导致理论与实践存在鸿沟。

Method: 基于ICA中变异因子可通过从数据点出发的径向曲线映射为潜在空间中的轴对齐直线这一观察，利用黎曼几何将全局生成模型替换为局部几何结构，引入解纠缠张量编码二阶点态解纠缠概念，该张量依赖于数据对数似然的Hessian矩阵及模型诱导的Ricci曲率。

Result: 在已知真实源的控制源恢复设置中，RICA在多个流形上成功恢复源，而ICA基线方法的成功与否依赖于表示观测数据的坐标系选择，证明了RICA的坐标无关性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究为不假设全局生成模型的局部解纠缠研究提供了理论基础，RICA填补了理论解纠缠与现代表示学习实践之间的空白，解纠缠张量作为核心贡献为分析点态解纠缠提供了几何视角。

Abstract: There is a gap between the theoretical foundations of disentanglement and the practice of modern representation learning. Existing theoretical frameworks, particularly Independent Component Analysis (ICA) and its nonlinear variants, assume a generative model with statistically independent latent variables underlying the data so that disentanglement amounts to identifying the latents that could have generated the data. This generative framework is interpretable and theoretically justified, but its strong assumptions make it difficult to apply to modern representation learning. Modern pretrained encoders often learn features that exhibit disentangled properties without making generative assumptions, yet there is no general theory for interpreting these features as independent factors of variation. We take a step toward such a theory by introducing Riemannian ICA (RICA), which replaces ICA's global generative model with local geometric structure. RICA is founded on the observation that in ICA, the factors of variation underlying a data point can be understood through radial curves emanating from the point that map to axis-aligned lines in the latent space. We formalize this perspective using Riemannian geometry and introduce our theory in a way that is consistent with the existing generative approach. Our main contribution is the disentanglement tensor, which encodes a second-order notion of disentanglement that we call pointwise disentanglement. This tensor depends on the Hessian of the data log likelihood as well as the Ricci curvature induced by the model. In a controlled source recovery setting with known ground-truth sources, RICA recovers sources across several manifolds, while the success of ICA baselines depends on the coordinates used to represent the observations. Our work provides a theoretical basis for studying local disentanglement without assuming a global generative model.

</details>


### [239] [Relational Linear Properties in Language Models: An Empirical Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22532)
*Giovanni Valer,Luigi Gresele,Marco Bronzini,Emanuele Marconato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于KL散度的高效探测方法，用于检验语言模型中“关系线性”假设（即固定关系下，客体嵌入可由主体嵌入通过线性映射预测），发现该性质在不同模型、层级和关系表述方式下存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性属性在语言模型表示中普遍存在，但实验验证仍具挑战性。现有方法（如Linear Relational Embeddings）依赖粗糙的Jacobian近似，效率低且不够精确。因此需要一种更高效、准确的方法来检验Marconato等人(2025)提出的关系线性假设。

Method: 提出基于Kullback-Leibler散度的探测方法来评估关系线性；该方法避免了先前工作中粗糙的Jacobian近似；系统考察了关系线性在不同层级和改写关系查询中的变化。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明：关系线性在不同模型间存在差异；呈现与先前关于模型表示中语言信息观察一致的层级模式；关系表述方式的改变会对其产生不同影响。

Conclusion: 关系线性是语言模型中一个可实证检验但非普遍恒定的性质，其表现受模型架构、表示层级和语言表述方式共同影响。所提出的KL散度探测方法为后续研究提供了更高效的分析工具。

Abstract: Linear properties are ubiquitous in the representations of language models; however, testing them experimentally remains a challenging task. This work focuses on relational linearity: the hypothesis that, for a fixed relation (e.g., "plays"), the unembedding of an object (e.g., "trumpet") can be predicted from the embedding of its subject (e.g.,"Miles Davis") by a linear map. We present an experimental method to test the formulation of relational linearity by Marconato et al. (2025). Specifically, we introduce a probing method, based on Kullback-Leibler divergence, to evaluate this property and examine its variation across layers and paraphrased relational queries. It is also more efficient than previous work; for example, it avoids the crude Jacobian approximations used in Linear Relational Embeddings by Hernandez et al. (2024). Our findings across four datasets show that relational linearity varies across models, exhibits layer-wise patterns consistent with prior observations about linguistic information in model representations, and is differently affected by changes in how the relation is phrased.

</details>


### [240] [F-TIS: Harnessing Diverse Models in Collaborative GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22537)
*Nikolay Blagoev,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Wendelin Boehmer,Lydia Yiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS)方法，解决了去中心化强化学习中异构模型协作训练的问题，实现了与同构策略相当的性能，并在某些情况下提升了12%的OOD泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO强化学习方法在分布式训练中假设同构模型，但在去中心化系统中，不同计算能力和偏好的参与方需要异构模型协作。然而，异构模型会导致高度离策略样本，已被证明会损害GRPO收敛性，因此需要能够处理异构模型的新方法。

Method: 提出Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS)框架，一种GRPO风格的训练范式，通过重要性采样技术利用离策略样本来改进本地模型学习，支持异构模型在同一强化学习训练中协作，同时保持通信高效性。

Result: 在多种异构设置中广泛评估F-TIS，表明该方法展现出与纯策略样本训练完全相同的最终模型收敛性；在部分设置中，OOD任务的泛化性能优于策略训练，性能提升高达12%。

Conclusion: F-TIS成功实现了去中心化环境下异构模型的高效协作训练，既保持了与同构训练相当的收敛性能，又在某些场景下获得了更好的泛化能力，为实际应用中的去中心化RL训练提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO have seen great popularity in LLM post-training. In GRPO, models produce completions to a set of prompts, which are rewarded, and the policy is updated towards the relatively high reward completions. Due to the auto-regressive nature of models, the generation phase of such style of training can be extremely time consuming. As a solution, prior work has sought to distribute the inference step across many nodes, working parallel. These works assume primarily homogeneous models in the training in order to keep samples as close to on-policy as possible. This assumption may be impractical in decentralized systems, where parties with various computes and preferences may wish to collaborate on the same task. Thus, decentralized training requires an approach that can handle heterogeneous models - different models collaborating on the same tasks. However, this leads to highly off-policy samples presented during training, which prior work has identified that off-policy samples can hurt GRPO convergence. To enable heterogeneity, we propose Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS) - a GRPO-style training paradigm that can use off-policy samples to improve local model's learning. Our framework allows various models to collaborate in the same RL training run while being communication efficient. We extensively evaluate F-TIS in various heterogeneous setups and we show that it exhibits identical final model convergence to purely on-sample training. Furthermore, we observe in some setups better generalization on out-of-distribution tasks than on-policy training, increasing model's performance by up to 12\%.

</details>


### [241] [ImplicitTerrainV2: Wavelet-Guided Spatially Adaptive Neural Terrain Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22556)
*Haoan Feng,Xin Xu,Leila De Floriani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ImplicitTerrainV2，一种紧凑高效的神经地形数据格式，通过小波复杂度场实现空间自适应频率控制，结合梯度匹配监督和后训练压缩技术，在保持高重建质量的同时大幅降低存储和训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统栅格数字高程模型(DEM)在非网格采样时依赖插值，在导数分析时使用有限差分算子，存在精度限制。现有隐式神经表示(INR)方法缺乏显式频率控制、忽视地形梯度结构，且模型过大、训练成本过高，难以实际部署。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1)小波复杂度场(WCF)，通过解析计算的小波系数生成空间自适应频率掩码，将高频容量定位到复杂地形区域；(2)复杂度感知的自适应采样策略，集中于高复杂度区域的训练；(3)梯度匹配监督以强化地形DEM的光滑流形结构；(4)后训练混合精度量化和熵编码压缩模型。

Result: 在50个瑞士地形瓦片上测试，端到端PSNR达到66.25 dB，相比先前工作提升5.70 dB；参数量减少3.2倍；单GPU训练时间仅55秒/瓦片；压缩后存储降至1.23 bpp，PSNR损失仅0.28 dB；在率失真性能上与多种主流DEM编解码器相当。

Conclusion: ImplicitTerrainV2成功将地形INR发展为紧凑高效的神经数据格式，不仅达到与传统编解码器竞争的率失真性能，还额外支持非网格点查询、闭式导数计算和分辨率无关重建，为下游GIS应用提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: Digital elevation models (DEMs) underpin terrain analysis in Geographic Information Systems (GIS), but in their common raster form, they rely on interpolation for off-grid sampling and finite-difference operators for derivative-based analysis. Implicit neural representations (INRs) offer a continuous alternative, but prior terrain INRs lack explicit frequency control, neglect the gradient structure of terrain, and remain too large and costly to train for practical deployment. We present ImplicitTerrainV2, which advances terrain INRs toward a compact, efficient neural terrain data format by combining a spectral control mechanism with wavelet-guided spatial adaptivity, derivative-aware supervision, and post-training model compression. At its core, a wavelet complexity field (WCF) derives spatially-adaptive frequency masks from analytically computed wavelet coefficients, localizing high-frequency capacity to complex terrain regions. The same field guides complexity-aware adaptive sampling that concentrates training in high-complexity regions, while gradient matching applies extra supervision to enforce the smooth manifold structure of terrain DEMs for improved derivative fidelity. Post-training mixed-precision quantization and entropy coding reduce storage to 1.23 bpp with a 0.28 dB PSNR drop. On 50 Swiss terrain tiles, ImplicitTerrainV2 reaches 66.25 dB end-to-end PSNR, improving over the prior work by 5.70 dB while using 3.2x fewer parameters and training in 55 s per tile on a single GPU. Our compressed neural format is competitive with several established DEM codecs in rate-distortion performance, while additionally supporting off-grid point queries, closed-form derivative evaluation, and resolution-independent reconstruction, which may benefit many downstream GIS applications.

</details>


### [242] [Neural Flow Operators can Approximate any Operator: Abstract Frameworks and Universal Approcimations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22557)
*Shuang Chen,Juncai He,Xue-Cheng Tai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个统一的抽象神经流框架，涵盖了有限维函数逼近和无限维算子逼近，证明了其适定性和通用逼近性质，并通过时间离散化方法建立了与ResNet和平面架构的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为神经网络和神经算子提供一个统一的连续深度建模框架，弥合有限维函数逼近与无限维算子逼近之间的差距，并为残差网络和平面架构提供统一的流模型理论基础。

Method: 构建了包含组合结构和分离结构两种连续深度模型的抽象神经流框架，通过时间离散化方法将组合结构恢复为ResNet型架构，将分离结构通过分裂离散化得到平面架构，支持全连接或卷积线性层。

Result: 证明了框架中神经流的适定性和通用逼近性质，首次获得了无限维空间之间基于流模型的通用逼近结果，以及卷积神经流模型的通用逼近结果。

Conclusion: 该框架成功统一了神经网络和神经算子的残差架构与平面架构，为流模型提供了完整的理论基础，包括首次在无限维算子逼近方面的重要理论突破。

Abstract: We introduce an abstract neural flow framework for neural networks and neural operators. The framework contains two continuous-depth models, namely neural flows with composition and separation structures, and covers both finite-dimensional function approximation and infinite-dimensional operator approximation. We prove well-posedness and universal approximation properties for the corresponding neural flows, including, to the best of our knowledge, the first universal approximation result for flow-based models between infinite-dimensional spaces. We also obtain universal approximation results for convolutional neural flow models. Through suitable time discretizations, the composition structure recovers ResNet-type architectures, while the separation structure, via a splitting-based discretization, yields plain architectures. This gives a unified flow-based route to both residual and plain architectures for neural networks and neural operators with fully connected or convolutional linear layers.

</details>


### [243] [Regret-Based $(ε,δ)$-optimal Stopping Criteria for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22561)
*Haowei Wang,Jingyi Wang,Qiyu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了更紧的GP-UCB瞬时遗憾界限，并基于此设计了首个具有理论保证的贝叶斯优化停止准则，确保在终止时以高概率获得ε-最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化通常在固定评估预算耗尽后终止，这可能导致不必要的计算成本且无法保证解的最优性质量。现有实用的停止准则虽有经验进展，但缺乏理论基础，亟需开发具有理论保证的停止准则。

Method: 首先推导了GP-UCB在任意迭代处的更紧瞬时遗憾界限；然后基于该界限提出了新的停止准则，通过理论分析证明该准则能在终止时以概率1-δ保证获得ε-最优解。

Result: 提出的停止准则具有严格的理论保证，数值实验验证了该准则的有效性和效率，能够在保证解质量的前提下避免不必要的计算开销。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了贝叶斯优化停止准则理论保证的空白，为实际应用提供了具有可靠性能保证的终止策略，推动了BO方法从经验实践向理论严谨性的发展。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a widely used iterative black-box optimization method that utilizes Gaussian process (GP) surrogate models. In practice, BO is typically terminated after a fixed evaluation budget is exhausted, which can incur unnecessary cost and provides no optimality guarantee on solution quality. Recent research in developing a practical stopping criterion has made empirical progress, yet a theoretically sound stopping criterion remains a work in progress. In this work, we present provably tighter instantaneous regret bounds for GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) at any given iteration. Then, we propose stopping criteria for GP-UCB based on this tighter bound that ensures an $ε$-optimal solution with high probability $1-δ$ upon termination. Numerical experiments are performed to validate and demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our stopping criteria.

</details>


### [244] [GraphFlow: A Graph-Based Workflow Management for Efficient LLM-Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22566)
*Ao Li,Shangpeng Yang,Fahao Chen,Tianheng Xu,Peng Li,Zhou Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GraphFlow系统，通过统一的图结构表示工作流（wGraph）来实现动态工作流生成和高效KV缓存管理，在五个基准数据集上平均性能提升约4.95个百分点，内存占用减少约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的工作流辅助智能体服务系统依赖预定义模板和浅层匹配机制，限制了系统捕获深层语义关系和泛化到未见过任务的能力。

Method: 提出基于wGraph的工作流管理范式：1）使用统一图结构表示工作流，节点对应原子操作；2）GraphFlow系统通过自适应工作流生成（基于任务语义和约束要求动态构建）和工作流状态管理（利用图结构高效管理KV缓存以减少冗余计算）两个关键设计实现。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明：相比最先进方法平均性能提升约4.95个百分点，内存占用减少约4倍。

Conclusion: 基于统一图表示的动态工作流生成和状态管理方法能有效提升LLM智能体服务的性能和资源效率。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents demonstrate strong reasoning and execution capabilities on complex tasks when guided by structured instructions, commonly referred to as workflows. However, existing workflow-assisted agent serving systems typically rely on predefined templates and shallow matching mechanisms, which limit their ability to capture deep semantic relationships and generalize to previously unseen tasks. To address these limitations, we propose a new workflow management paradigm that represents workflows using a unified graph, termed wGraph, where each node corresponds to an atomic operation. wGraph serves as a shared substrate from which task-specific workflows are dynamically instantiated. Building on wGraph primitives, we introduce GraphFlow, a system that efficiently integrates workflows into agent serving through two key designs. First, adaptive workflow generation dynamically constructs workflows from wGraph based on task semantics and constraint requirements. Second, workflow state management exploits wGraph structure to efficiently manage Key-Value (KV) caches, reducing redundant computation during agent serving. Extensive experiments across five benchmark datasets show that GraphFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, yielding an average performance improvement of approximately 4.95 percentage points, while achieving an approximately 4$\times$ reduction in memory footprint.

</details>


### [245] [A Tutorial on Diffusion Theory: From Differential Equations to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22586)
*Jiayi Fu,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本教程从微分方程视角系统构建扩散模型理论框架，揭示了正向/逆向随机微分方程(SDE)与常微分方程(ODE)的数学基础，证明了标准噪声预测目标与分数匹配的等价性，并统一了DDPM和DDIM等采样方法的理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型教程多从离散视角或概率视角出发，缺乏统一的微分方程理论框架。本文旨在从微分方程视角重新构建扩散模型理论，揭示不同扩散模型变体（如DDPM、DDIM）之间的内在联系，为理解各种训练目标和采样方法提供统一的数学基础。

Method: 从条件高斯正向过程出发，证明其同时具有ODE表示和SDE表示；通过对数据分布边缘化得到正向ODE/SDE公式；推导逆向时间动力学，包括逆向SDE和逆向概率流ODE，两者均由边缘分数函数控制；建立分数估计的训练目标，证明标准噪声预测目标与分数匹配等价；讨论采样方法（DPM-Solver、分类器引导、无分类器引导）及DDPM/DDIM与逆向SDE/ODE框架的对应关系。

Result: 建立了扩散模型完整的微分方程理论框架，揭示了：1)正向过程具有ODE和SDE双重表示；2)逆向动力学由边缘分数函数统一控制；3)噪声预测目标与分数匹配目标等价；4)DDPM采样对应离散逆向SDE采样，DDIM采样对应逆向ODE采样；5)所有方法共享相同的训练目标。

Conclusion: 微分方程视角为扩散模型提供了统一的理论框架，不仅揭示了DDPM、DDIM等方法内在联系，还为设计新的采样算法（如DPM-Solver）和引导采样技术提供了理论基础。该框架有助于深入理解扩散模型的工作原理，并为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: This tutorial develops diffusion models from the viewpoint of differential equations. We begin with the conditional Gaussian forward process and show that this path admits both an ordinary differential equation (ODE) representation and a stochastic differential equation (SDE) representation. Averaging the conditional process over the data distribution then yields marginalized forward ODE and SDE formulations that transport the data distribution $p_0=p_{\mathrm{data}}$ to a Gaussian prior $p_1=\mathcal{N}(0,I)$. We next derive the corresponding reverse-time dynamics, namely the reverse SDE and the reverse probability-flow ODE, both of which are governed by the marginal score $\grad\log p_t(x)$. This leads to a training objective for score estimation and shows that the standard noise-prediction objective is equivalent to score matching up to an additive constant independent of the model parameters. We then discuss sampling methods for the learned reverse dynamics, including DPM-Solver, as well as guided sampling through classifier guidance and classifier-free guidance. Finally, we compare DDPM and DDIM with the reverse SDE/ODE framework and show that they share the same training objective, while DDPM sampling corresponds to discrete reverse-SDE sampling and DDIM sampling corresponds to reverse-ODE sampling.

</details>


### [246] [Do Deep Ensembles Actually Capture Uncertainty in Graph Neural Networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22593)
*Pedro C. Vieira,Pedro Ribeiro,Viacheslav Borovitskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度集成方法在图神经网络中效果不佳，主要表现为认知坍塌现象：独立训练的模型收敛到过于相似的预测，导致集成失去了捕获认知不确定性的关键优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度集成方法在计算机视觉等领域被广泛认为是深度学习中不确定性量化的默认方法，但其在图结构数据上的有效性往往只是基于其他领域成功经验的假设，缺乏专门的验证和研究。

Method: 研究者在七个代表不同任务和复杂度的数据集上，对消息传递图神经网络的深度集成方法进行了基准测试。通过随机-认知不确定性分解分析，深入研究了集成方法的性能表现及其背后的机制。

Result: 实验发现深度集成在图数据上仅比单模型提供极有限的改进。这些边际收益主要来自稳定点预测中的优化噪声，而非提供有意义的不确定性估计。关键发现是认知坍塌现象：独立训练的网络收敛到过度相似的预测，而多样性正是集成捕获认知不确定性的基本机制。进一步分析表明，这种坍塌由函数空间而非权重空间的凸性驱动。

Conclusion: 深度集成方法的成功并不能无缝迁移到图机器学习领域，其在图神经网络中的局限性揭示了不同数据结构和学习范式对不确定性量化方法的影响，为图学习领域的不确定性量化研究提供了重要见解。

Abstract: While deep ensembles are widely considered to be the default method for uncertainty quantification in deep learning, their effectiveness for graph-structured data is often simply assumed based on successes in domains like computer vision. We investigate standard deep ensembles specifically for message-passing graph neural networks. Benchmarking across seven datasets representing varied tasks and complexities, we reveal that ensembles provide surprisingly little improvement over a single model. Instead, the observed marginal gains stem primarily from stabilizing optimization noise in point predictions rather than yielding meaningfully better uncertainty estimates. Through an aleatoric-epistemic decomposition, we identify epistemic collapse: independently trained networks consistently converge to overly similar predictions. Because disagreement is the fundamental mechanism through which ensembles capture epistemic uncertainty, this lack of diversity neutralizes their key advantage. Analyzing this phenomenon further, we suggest this collapse is driven by functional rather than weight-space convexity, where distinct parameter solutions induce almost identical behavior. Our results suggest that deep ensemble success does not seamlessly transfer to graph machine learning.

</details>


### [247] [Factored Diffusion Policies:Compositionally Generalized Robot Control with a Single Score Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22596)
*Sayan Mitra,Ege Yuceel,Noah Giles,Abhishek Pai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了分解扩散策略（Factored Diffusion Policies），通过单个共享扩散网络和零令牌丢弃训练，将机器人任务的训练数据需求从组合爆炸降低为线性求和，并提供了理论误差界限和实际验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务通常由多个因素（如目标物体、障碍物、颜色等）的元组指定。为每个因素值组合收集专家演示数据会产生组合爆炸问题，导致训练成本不可承受。

Method: 提出分解扩散策略：使用单个共享扩散网络，采用每因素零令牌丢弃（null-token dropout）训练。在近似条件独立假设下，推理时分数可跨因素加性分解，近似真实联合分数。引入轨迹管道证书（trajectory-tube certificate），将分数级误差界限通过反向采样ODE和收缩跟踪控制器传播到闭环状态轨迹。

Result: 在无人机竞速实验中：基于状态的多门竞速任务，分解策略通过90%的留出门测试（与Oracle匹配），而K网络组合基线仅3%；基于视觉的单门穿越任务，零样本迁移到未见场景时成功率提升11.7个百分点，坠毁率降低2.4倍。

Conclusion: 分解扩散策略成功将训练任务预算从因素基数的乘积降低为求和，提供了有界均匀误差的理论保证，并通过实验验证了泛化界限和证书的有效性，在保持性能的同时显著降低了数据需求。

Abstract: Robotic tasks are typically specified by a tuple of factors, such as the object to be grasped, the obstacles to be avoided, the color of the target, and so on. Collecting expert demonstrations for every combination of factor values grows combinatorially. We present factored diffusion policies: a single shared diffusion network trained with per-factor null-token dropout, whose score decomposes additively across factors at inference. Under approximate conditional independence between factors given the action-observation pair, this composition approximates the true joint score with a bounded uniform error, reducing the training-task budget from a product of factor cardinalities to a sum. A trajectory-tube certificate chains this score-level bound through the reverse-time sampling ODE and a contracting tracking controller into a closed-loop state-trajectory tube whose radius factors into an ODE-sensitivity constant and a per-factor score-error budget. Unlike compositional-diffusion methods for control that combine separately trained networks, we use one shared network. Drone racing experiments confirm both the generalization bound and the certificate. On state-based multi-gate racing, the factored policy passes 90% of held-out gates -- matching an oracle -- while a K-network composition baseline collapses to 3%; on vision-based single-gate traversal, it transfers zero-shot to an unseen venue with +11.7pp success-rate gain and 2.4X crash-rate reduction.

</details>


### [248] [Benchmarking Machine Learning Architectures for Antimicrobial Stewardship in Pediatric ICUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22611)
*Niklas Raehse,Luregn J. Schlapbach,Daphné Chopard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在儿科重症监护室（PICU）中系统评估了机器学习预测抗菌药物管理（AMS）干预的性能。四种临床相关目标（静脉转口服、降级治疗、停药和短程治疗）的预测性能主要取决于目标流行率和数据集特征，而非模型复杂度；序列模型在粗粒度时间分辨率上表现较优但校准性较差，表格模型则提供更可靠的预测概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PICU中诊断不确定性常导致广谱抗生素的过度使用，加剧抗菌药物耐药性和潜在长期危害。机器学习虽可用于从电子健康记录数据中识别患者层面的管理干预机会，但现有研究主要集中在成人群体和静态表格表示上，缺乏在儿科人群中的系统性基准测试。

Method: 研究定义了四种临床相关的减少抗生素暴露的代理目标：静脉转口服、降级治疗、停药和短程治疗。在统一评估框架下，对比了表格模型、序列模型和基于图的时序模型在多个时间分辨率下的表现，研究覆盖了公共数据集和私有机构队列。

Result: 预测性能主要由目标流行率和数据集特征决定，模型复杂度的影响较小。序列模型在粗粒度（24小时）分辨率下比表格方法提高了精确率-召回率的权衡，但在更细粒度的时序建模上收益有限。然而，这些提升以校准性下降为代价，更简单的表格模型产生了更可靠的概率估计。多任务学习仅带来边际改进，表明管理目标间的共享结构有限。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了目标设计、时序表示和校准在临床机器学习中的重要性，为开发可靠的儿科抗菌药物管理决策支持系统提供了实用指导。在临床场景中，需要在预测性能与模型可解释性、校准性之间寻求平衡。

Abstract: Antimicrobial stewardship (AMS) is critical in pediatric intensive care units (PICUs), where diagnostic uncertainty often drives broad-spectrum antibiotic use, increasing antimicrobial resistance and potential long-term harms. Machine learning offers a promising approach for identifying patient-level opportunities for stewardship interventions from electronic health record data, yet prior work has focused largely on adult populations and static tabular representations. We present a systematic benchmarking study of AMS intervention prediction in the PICU across a public dataset and a private institutional cohort. We define four clinically relevant proxy targets for reducing antibiotic exposure: intravenous-to-oral switching, de-escalation, discontinuation, and short-course therapy. Under a unified evaluation framework, we compare tabular, sequence-based, and graph-based temporal models at multiple temporal resolutions. We find that predictive performance is driven primarily by target prevalence and dataset characteristics rather than model complexity. Sequence models improve the precision-recall trade-off over tabular approaches at coarse (24-hour) resolution, while finer temporal modeling provides limited additional benefit. However, these gains come at the cost of poorer calibration, with simpler tabular models yielding more reliable probability estimates. Multi-task learning produces only marginal improvements, suggesting limited shared structure across stewardship targets. Our findings highlight the importance of target design, temporal representation, and calibration in clinical machine learning, and provide practical guidance for developing reliable decision support systems for pediatric AMS.

</details>


### [249] [Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization for LLM-Guided Program Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22613)
*Halil Alperen Gozeten,Xuechen Zhang,Emrullah Ildiz,Ege Onur Taga,Tara Javidi,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EMO-STA框架，通过先演化共享程序库再适配目标任务的两阶段方法，在多任务LLM程序发现中提升计算效率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的进化搜索方法针对每个任务独立优化，忽略了相关任务之间可复用的结构信息，导致计算资源浪费和潜在泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出EMO-STA（Shared-Then-Adapt）两阶段框架：第一阶段在任务族上演化共享可执行程序库，第二阶段将选定的共享候选程序适配到各目标任务。探索多种适配策略，包括从共享库热启动、适配平均最佳共享程序、以及适配在各目标任务上表现最佳的共享程序。

Result: 在涵盖连续优化、几何构造、建模和算法优化的八个任务族上，EMO-STA在大多数设置下优于等计算量的单任务进化。STA Best-Local提供最强的分布内适配，STA Best-Shared对未见任务具有鲁棒迁移能力。计算分配实验表明将大部分预算分配给共享演化是有益的，平衡的共享与适配预算通常最优。共享演化还能在低证据场景（如ARC任务、时间序列特征工程）中缓解过拟合。

Conclusion: 跨任务共享程序演化能显著提升LLM引导程序发现的计算效率和泛化能力，平衡的计算分配策略最优，且共享演化可有效缓解低数据场景下的过拟合问题。

Abstract: Recent LLM-guided evolutionary search methods have shown that iterative program mutation can discover strong algorithms, but they typically optimize each task independently, even when related tasks share reusable structure. We introduce Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization (EMO) for LLM-guided program discovery, and propose EMO-STA (Shared-Then-Adapt), a two-stage framework that first evolves a shared archive of executable programs across a task family and then adapts selected shared candidates to each target task. Within EMO-STA, we explore multiple adaptation strategies, including warm-starting from the shared archive, adapting the best average shared program, and adapting the shared program that performs best on each target task. Across eight task families spanning continuous optimization, geometric construction, modeling, and algorithmic optimization, EMO-STA improves over matched-compute single-task evolution in most settings, with STA Best-Local providing the strongest in-distribution adaptation and STA Best-Shared yielding robust transfer to unseen tasks. Compute-allocation experiments show that allocating a substantial fraction of the family-level budget to shared evolution is consistently beneficial, with roughly balanced shared and adaptation budgets often being optimal. Beyond compute efficiency, we show that shared evolution can mitigate overfitting in low-evidence settings (e.g. few training data), including ARC tasks and time-series feature engineering, by favoring programs that generalize across all tasks rather than exploiting task-specific brittle artifacts.

</details>


### [250] [Two is better than one: A Collapse-free Multi-Reward RLIF Training Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22620)
*Shourov Joarder,Diganta Sikdar,Ahsan Habib Akash,Binod Bhattarai,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多内部奖励的强化学习框架（多奖励RLIF），通过结合答案级和补全级奖励并引入新的正则化方法，在不依赖外部监督的情况下实现了与有监督方法相近的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督强化学习方法（RLIF）通常依赖单一内部奖励信号，容易导致奖励欺骗、熵崩溃以及推理结构退化问题，因此需要更稳定可靠的内部奖励机制。

Method: 提出多奖励RLIF框架，将训练信号分解为两部分：基于聚类投票的答案级奖励和基于token自确定性的补全级奖励。采用GDPO归一化方法平衡奖励尺度，并引入KL-Cov正则化来防止低熵token分布导致的熵崩溃。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试中，该方法相比现有无监督RL方法在稳定性和鲁棒性上有显著提升，性能接近有监督的RLVR方法。

Conclusion: 互补的内部奖励信号结合针对性正则化可以在不依赖外部监督的情况下支持稳定的长时序推理，为无监督强化学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the reasoning ability of LLMs, but often depends on external supervision from human annotations or gold-standard solutions. Reinforcement learning from internal feedback (RLIF) has recently emerged as a scalable unsupervised alternative, using signals extracted from the model itself. However, existing RLIF methods typically rely on a single internal reward, which can lead to reward hacking, entropy collapse, and degraded reasoning structure. We propose a multi-reward RLIF framework that decomposes the training signal into two complementary components: an answer-level reward based on cluster voting and a completion-level reward based on token-wise self-certainty. To combine these signals robustly, we apply GDPO-based normalization to reduce reward-scale imbalance. We further introduce KL-Cov regularization, which targets low-entropy token distributions responsible for disproportionate entropy reduction, preserving exploration and preventing late-stage collapse. Across mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, our method improves stability and robustness over prior unsupervised RL approaches, while achieving performance close to supervised RLVR methods. These results show that complementary internal rewards, combined with targeted regularization, can support stable long-horizon reasoning without relying on external ground-truth supervision. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [251] [A note on convergence of Wasserstein policy optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22622)
*David Šiška,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了WPO算法在熵正则化MDP框架下具有线性收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WPO算法在连续动作空间的强化学习中取得了实证成功，但其在连续状态和动作空间环境中的理论收敛性质尚未完全建立。

Method: 利用平均场分析和log-Sobolev不等式研究梯度流收敛性；假设梯度流方程存在足够正则的解，证明沿流方向的能量单调耗散，并建立局部log-Sobolev不等式。

Result: 证明了价值函数可以线性收敛到全局最优解。

Conclusion: WPO算法在熵正则化马尔可夫决策过程框架下具有线性收敛性，为该算法提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Wasserstein Policy Optimization (WPO) is a recently proposed reinforcement learning algorithm that leverages Wasserstein gradient flows to optimize stochastic policies in continuous action spaces. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical convergence properties of WPO in environments with continuous state and action spaces have yet to be fully established. In this note, we argue that WPO within the framework of entropy-regularised Markov Decision Processes converges linearly. This is done by leveraging recent advances in mean-field analysis for convergence of gradient flows using log-Sobole inequalities. Assuming existence of sufficiently regular solution to the gradient flow equation we demonstrate monotonic energy dissipation along the flow and establish a local log-Sobolev inequality. Ultimately, these properties allow us to argue that the value function should converge linearly to the global optimum.

</details>


### [252] [The Double Dilemma in Multi-Task Radiology Report Generation: A Gradient Dynamics Analysis and Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22635)
*Erjian Zhang,Yatong Hao,Liejun Wang,Zhiqing Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAME-Grad优化器，通过冲突规避方向校正和幅度增强能量注入解决了多任务学习中线性标量化策略的梯度动态问题，在放射学报告生成任务上实现了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于多任务学习的放射学报告生成方法主要关注架构设计，但在优化策略上仍局限于粗糙的线性标量化方法。这些方法无法有效平衡判别性临床监督的硬约束与报告生成的平滑性要求，导致临床一致性难以保证。

Method: 从梯度动力学角度分析线性标量化的失效机制，利用随机微分方程(SDE)框架将其表征为漂移项偏差和扩散项衰减的"双重困境"。基于此提出CAME-Grad优化器：1) 通过冲突规避方向校正确保几何有效性；2) 通过幅度增强能量注入避免局部最优；3) 使用自适应梯度融合机制在理论最优方向和任务特定归纳偏置之间建立动态平衡。

Result: 作为通用的即插即用优化器，CAME-Grad在8种不同的放射学报告生成方法上带来了实质性和一致性的改进。在MIMIC-CXR数据集上平均提升2.3%的临床效能表现，在IU X-Ray数据集上平均提升1.9%。

Conclusion: CAME-Grad作为一种骨干网络无关的优化器，成功解决了多任务学习中临床监督与报告生成之间的平衡问题，为放射学报告生成领域提供了一个通用且有效的优化解决方案。

Abstract: While multi-task learning based automatic radiology report generation (RRG) is widely adopted to ensure clinical consistency, most focus on architectural designs yet remain limited to coarse linear scalarization strategies. These strategies cannot effectively balance the hard constraints of discriminative clinical supervision with the smoothness requirements of report generation. To address these problems, we analyze the failure mechanism of linear scalarization from the perspective of gradient dynamics, utilizing the stochastic differential equation (SDE) framework to characterize it as a "Double Dilemma" of drift term deviation and diffusion term decay. Based on this, we propose a backbone-agnostic optimizer named Conflict-Averse Magnitude-Enhanced Gradient Descent (CAME-Grad). Through conflict-averse direction rectification and magnitude-enhanced energy injection, the algorithm not only ensures geometric validity, but also avoids local optimal solutions. Then, the adaptive gradient fusion mechanism is used to establish a dynamic balance between the theoretical optimal direction and the task-specific inductive bias. Experiments show that as a universal plug-and-play optimizer, CAME-Grad brings substantial and consistent improvements across eight diverse RRG methods, elevating overall clinical efficacy performance by an average of 2.3\% on MIMIC-CXR and 1.9\% on IU X-Ray. Our code is available at https://github.com/vpsg-research/CAME-Grad.

</details>


### [253] [Why SGD is not Brownian Motion: A New Perspective on Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22644)
*Igor Ignashin,Anna Radovskaya,Andrew Semenov,Egor Lopatin,Stanislav Potapov,Aleksandr Kovalenko,Andrey Veprikov,Aleksandr Shestakov,Andrey Leonidov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了随机梯度下降(SGD)的另一种理论框架，将其建模为小批量采样引起的波动损失景观中的确定性动力学，而非传统的朗之万过程；推导出的离散Fokker-Planck方程与标准朗之万形式在η²阶存在差异，并发现平坦方向上不存在平稳分布，而是表现出有效扩散行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将SGD建模为朗之万过程，假设小批量噪声作为布朗运动，但这种近似依赖于连续时间极限和√η噪声缩放，在有限学习率的离散SGD更新中并不匹配。因此需要建立一个更准确的理论框架，直接从离散更新出发描述SGD动力学。

Method: 提出SGD在小批量采样引起的波动损失景观中的确定性动力学表述；从离散更新出发推导参数分布的主方程；获得离散Fokker-Planck方程，分析其在η²阶与标准朗之万形式的差异；沿平均Hessian矩阵的特征基分析临界点附近的SGD动力学。

Result: 发现SGD动力学沿平均Hessian的特征基分解为不同定性区域；平坦方向不存在平稳分布，方差随时间增长，对应沿谷的有效扩散，扩散系数与学习率成正比；在计算机视觉和自然语言处理的神经网络模型上验证了这些预测，观察到受限模式和扩散模式之间清晰的定性分离。

Conclusion: SGD的离散Fokker-Planck表述提供了比传统朗之万近似更准确的理论框架，揭示了损失景观平坦方向上的扩散行为，这对理解SGD在神经网络训练中的隐式正则化和泛化性质具有重要意义。

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) is commonly modeled as a Langevin process, assuming that minibatch noise acts as Brownian motion. However, this approximation relies on a continuous-time limit and a sqrt(eta) noise scaling that does not match the discrete SGD update at finite learning rate. In this work, we propose an alternative formulation of SGD as deterministic dynamics in a fluctuating loss landscape induced by minibatch sampling. Starting directly from the discrete update, we derive a master equation for the parameter distribution and obtain a discrete Fokker--Planck equation that differs from the standard Langevin form at order eta^2. Using this framework, we analyze SGD dynamics near critical points of the loss. We show that the behavior decomposes along the eigenbasis of the mean Hessian into qualitatively distinct regimes. In particular, nearly-flat directions do not admit a stationary distribution: the variance grows over time, corresponding to effective diffusion along valleys with a coefficient proportional to the learning rate. We provide empirical evidence supporting these predictions on neural network models in computer vision and natural language processing, observing a clear qualitative separation between confined and diffusive modes.

</details>


### [254] [ChronoVAE-HOPE: Beyond Attention -- A Next-Generation VAE Foundation Model for Specialized Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22684)
*José Alberto Rodríguez,Luis Balderas,Miguel Lastra,Antonio Arauzo-Azofra,José M. Benítez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ChronoVAE-HOPE模型，通过双记忆系统替代二次注意力机制，并引入解耦潜在空间分离趋势与季节成分，在时间序列分类任务上实现高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型(TSFMs)在适应专门化分类任务时面临两大挑战：标准注意力机制的二次计算成本，以及无法解耦时间序列变异性背后的结构成分。

Method: 构建基于HOPE Block的变分自编码器(VAE)框架：(1)采用双记忆系统替代二次注意力——Titans模块处理动态短期记忆，Continuum Memory System(CMS)处理长期历史上下文；(2)设计解耦潜在空间，通过专用编码器头和独立解码器路径将表征因子化为独立的趋势和季节成分；(3)在Monash数据集上进行自监督预训练，结合掩码时间序列建模(MTSM)辅助目标与解耦VAE重建损失；(4)冻结预训练编码器，在UCR基准数据集上进行下游分类。

Result: 在多样化时间域上展现出强劲性能，特别是在具有严格因果结构的场景中表现突出。验证了该方法在时间序列分类任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: ChronoVAE-HOPE通过结构化生成表示建立了一个稳健且可解释的框架，成功实现了基础模型向时间序列分类任务的适配，为时间序列基础模型的专业化应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have become a new component of the state-of-the-art in general time series forecasting. However, adapting them to specialized classification tasks remains constrained by two interconnected challenges: the quadratic cost of standard attention mechanisms and the inability to disentangle the structural components underlying time series variability. This technical report introduces ChronoVAE-HOPE, a next-generation TSFM that reconciles massive generalization with structured latent representation for time series classification. The core of the proposal is a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) framework built upon the HOPE Block, which replaces quadratic attention with a dual-memory system: Titans modules for dynamic short-term retention and a Continuum Memory System (CMS) for the abstraction of long-term historical context. A key architectural novelty is the disentangled latent space, which factorizes representations into independent trend and seasonal components via dedicated encoder heads and separate decoder pathways. ChronoVAE-HOPE undergoes self-supervised pre-training on the Monash archive, combining a Masked Time Series Modeling (MTSM) auxiliary objective with a disentangled VAE reconstruction loss. The pre-trained encoder is subsequently frozen and used to generate fixed-length embeddings for downstream classification on the UCR benchmark datasets. Empirical results demonstrate strong performance across diverse temporal domains, particularly in settings characterized by strict causal structure. ChronoVAE-HOPE establishes a robust and interpretable framework for the adaptation of foundation models to time series classification through structured generative representations.

</details>


### [255] [Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22691)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了β-VAE中的后验塌缩机制实现了自动谱剪枝，塌缩阈值由β设定，并提供了识别有效潜在变量的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解β-VAE中后验塌缩现象的本质机制，以及超参数β如何影响潜在变量的激活与塌缩。

Method: 采用Landau稳定性分析推导塌缩条件，定义潜在重缩放不变序参量来排序活跃潜在模式，在WorldClim数据集上验证理论预测。

Result: 后验塌缩实现了按效用排序的自动谱剪枝：β值增大时潜在模式按效用从低到高依次塌缩；线性高斯情况下，塌缩谱、效用谱与归一化PCA谱重合，每个塌缩遵循平均场定律。

Conclusion: β-VAE的后验塌缩是一种有原则的自动模型简化机制，塌缩阈值可用于识别最值得检查的有效变量。

Abstract: We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful.
  We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first.
  In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.

</details>


### [256] [Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22703)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Chiyu Ma,Kexin Huang,Haoming Meng,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Guoyin Wang,Li Yuan,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR)方法，通过在裁剪边界进行随机扰动来解决RLVR训练中的硬裁剪瓶颈问题，在不同规模和架构的模型上均取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）在LLM推理扩展中发挥核心作用，但其优化过程常面临训练不稳定和收敛次优的问题。通过系统剖析基于裁剪的GRPO风格目标函数，作者发现硬裁剪导致的刚性裁剪决策是关键瓶颈——边界附近区域存在有价值信号，但被标准硬裁剪规则丢弃。

Method: 提出Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR)：一种最小化、即插即用的改进方法，通过随机采样机制在边界处随机保留略微超出边界的token，从而恢复丢失的信号。该方法可解释为在期望意义上诱导隐式梯度衰减，但其随机性、边界局部救援机制比确定性梯度衰减更有效。

Result: 在7B至30B模型规模、稠密架构和MoE架构上进行了广泛实验验证。作为即插即用方案，NSR显著改善了训练稳定性，并在DAPO和GSPO等强基线方法上取得一致的增益。

Conclusion: NSR通过简单的随机边界扰动有效解决了硬裁剪的刚性问题，证明了边界附近信号的价值。该方法易于集成，在多种模型规模和架构上均表现出良好的泛化性和有效性，为RLVR训练稳定性提供了一个实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.

</details>


### [257] [Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22719)
*Mahdi Nasermoghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对GPT-2小型模型在间接对象识别（IOI）任务上的失败案例进行了稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征审计，发现最强失败相关特征与"密钥"对象相关，但通过三项控制实验验证该特征仅为相关而非因果因素，主要贡献是提出了一种低成本、模型无关的审计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在识别和验证语言模型任务失败的可解释性特征，探索SAE特征是否能揭示模型行为的因果机制，并为可解释性研究建立方法论标准。

Method: 使用Bloom (2024)发布的第8层残差流SAE，在300个提示上分析GPT-2小型模型在IOI任务失败与成功时的特征激活差异，应用Holm校正显著性阈值和Cohen's d效应量筛选特征，并通过三项控制实验验证：因果消融、表征基线比较、种子稳健性检查。

Result: 在24,576个特征中发现146个显著特征，105个大效应特征。最强失败相关特征（17,491号，标记为"cryptographic keys"）在提示对象为"the keys"时几乎全部激活，模型失败率达93.3%。然而消融实验显示该特征并非充分原因，原始残差流基线模型与SAE特征预测性能相当（ROC AUC ≈ 0.93），且该特征在5个随机种子中仅有1次为最强相关特征。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要贡献在于提出了一个低成本、可在笔记本电脑上运行的审计流程，而非发现具有因果机制的单个特征。SAE特征增加了可解释性但未提升预测能力，强调了相关性与因果性之间的区分以及严格方法论控制在可解释性研究中的重要性。

Abstract: We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).

</details>


### [258] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多任务共享设定下算子集合的学习复杂度，证明了多任务算子学习的扩展规律与单算子学习相同，共享表征不会增加总体成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在多任务共享设定下学习算子集合的逼近和统计复杂度，重点关注多神经算子（MNO）架构，以理解共享表征是否会影响多任务学习的整体成本。

Method: 针对广泛的Lipschitz多算子映射类，推导逼近和统计泛化的近优上界；在理论下界方面，建立参数复杂度诅咒并证明相应的极小极大速率；将MNO与基于拼接任务输入的DeepONet多任务扩展进行比较分析。

Result: 证明了多任务算子学习遵循与单算子学习相同的扩展规律，共享表征不会增加总体成本；从最坏情况逼近复杂度角度看，MNO与多任务DeepONet架构满足本质上相同的渐近速率。

Conclusion: 多任务算子学习中的共享表征不会带来额外的复杂度代价，MNO架构在理论复杂度方面表现良好，为多任务算子学习的可行性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [259] [Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22740)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三元决策树，通过在分裂节点引入不确定区域实现软划分，在72个数据集上显著优于传统CART方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树使用硬二值阈值划分特征空间，对远离决策边界和边界上的实例赋予相同的置信度，无法区分预测的确定性程度，缺乏对边界不确定性的建模能力。

Method: 在分裂节点引入半宽为δ的不确定区域，位于该区域的实例通过加权混合双子树进行预测，并标记为边界不确定。提出了五种δ估计方法：质量平台、类别重叠、增益比、节点自助采样和边界距离，无需外部噪声参数或额外超参数。

Result: 在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上，五种方法在确定准确率上均显著优于CART（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p<0.001）。边界距离方法效率最高（每单位边界不确定标记率获得0.104准确率增益），在42/72数据集上获胜。在医学和金融数据集上，节点自助采样方法在乳腺X光检查中通过标记10.8%病例为边界不确定，提升了0.71%的确定准确率。

Conclusion: 三元决策树通过局部不确定性量化有效提升了预测可靠性和可解释性，边界距离方法表现最优且无需额外超参数，节点自助采样和质量平台方法能很好地跟踪理论不可约误差，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example).
