Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.

</details>


### [260] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SeqLoRA框架，通过双层优化联合优化LoRA因子，在无需昂贵融合的情况下实现了多达101个概念的可组合生成，显著提升了身份保持能力和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法在组合多个自定义概念时面临表示干扰问题。现有模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后验融合，要么冻结适应子空间，这限制了表达能力和概念保真度。需要在避免这些限制的同时实现多概念的有效组合。

Method: 提出Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA)框架，这是一个受约束的持续学习框架。该方法通过双层优化（bilevel optimization）联合优化两个LoRA因子。理论上将残差层激活建模为矩阵次高斯过程，以推导灾难性遗忘的高概率边界，并证明了从数据学习LoRA基比冻结基方法能更有效地最小化残差干扰能量。

Result: 在多概念图像生成实验中，SeqLoRA在身份保持和可扩展性方面表现出色，能够扩展至多达101个概念。该方法避免了昂贵的融合操作，同时减少了组合生成中的属性干扰。理论上建立了算法的强收敛保证和灾难性遗忘的高概率边界。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA成功解决了参数高效微调中多概念组合的核心挑战，在理论收敛性保证和实际性能上都取得了显著进展。该方法为文本到图像扩散模型的个性化定制提供了一个可扩展、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [261] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化的证据深度学习(EDL)目标函数，通过在Dirichlet均值处使用插件损失来近似原始目标，在保持预测准确性和选择性预测性能的同时，简化了实现复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中基于传感器的学习系统需要既可靠又计算高效的不确定性估计。证据深度学习(EDL)通过Dirichlet分布建模类别概率提供单次传递的不确定性估计，但该方法面临计算挑战，因为Dirichlet期望目标比标准监督学习损失更复杂，难以分析和实现。

Method: 通过在Dirichlet均值处评估的插件损失来近似EDL诱导的一阶经验风险最小化问题的目标函数。在温和假设下，对于包括均方误差和交叉熵损失在内的广泛损失函数类别，证明了近似误差随着证据的增长而衰减。在特定证据到Dirichlet映射下，该框架包含了标准softmax分类器。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands数据集上验证了所提出的简化目标函数，实现了与经典EDL相当的预测准确性和选择性预测性能，同时可以使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程更简单地实现。首次在语音识别任务中通过EDL获得了覆盖率-准确性权衡结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为在不确定性估计中使用softmax提供了理论依据，简化了EDL的实现，使其更容易在标准深度学习流程中部署，为语音识别等领域的不确定性估计提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [262] [Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22756)
*Christian Janos Lebeda,David Erb,Tudor Cebere,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Lumberjack算法，一种新的差分隐私随机森林方法，通过构建大型随机决策树并应用隐私保护剪枝，显著提高了隐私-效用权衡，达到了新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私随机森林方法通常会严重降低性能，以至于在实际应用中不可行。因此，需要一种新的方法，能够在保护隐私的同时保持高实用性。

Method: 提出Lumberjack算法，通过构建大型随机决策树，然后应用激进的、隐私保护的剪枝方法，仅保留充分填充的节点。此外，设计了一种新颖的$(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP重击检测算法，用于层次数据，其误差为$O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$，适用于高度为$h$的树。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实证评估表明，Lumberjack算法在隐私-效用权衡方面显著优于之前的差分隐私随机森林方法，达到了新的最先进水平。特别是在实际隐私预算下，取得了显著的改进。

Conclusion: 精心设计的差分隐私随机森林方法可以缩小实用性差距，为未来研究提供了一个有前景且未被充分探索的方向。

Abstract: Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

</details>


### [263] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了均匀扩散模型(UDM)中标准参数化与训练目标的不匹配问题，提出了留一后验参数化和吸收态重构方法，显著提升了生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型通常通过干净数据预测进行训练，但不同的反向动态定义方式会导致不同结果。在掩码扩散模型(MDM)中这些选择基本一致，但在均匀扩散模型(UDM)中却不一致。作者发现UDM的标准插件桥参数化存在理论缺陷，与常用的交叉熵去噪目标不匹配，需要深入理解并解决这一问题。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明了UDM的标准插件桥参数化不是由去噪后验优化，而是由留一后验优化（预测每个干净token时不使用其自身的噪声观测）。
2. 推导转换关系：建立了去噪器、留一后验和分数之间的精确转换公式，实现了参数化与训练目标的解耦。
3. 推理改进：提出了基于留一预测器的信息预测-校正采样器和改进的温度采样方法，无需额外训练。
4. 吸收态重构：引入均匀扩散的吸收态重新表述，保持UDM联合律同时将其分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作，具有更简单的去噪后验、延续去掩码机制和自然的重新掩码机制。

Result: 1. 发现并纠正了UDM参数化与训练目标的理论不匹配。
2. 基于留一参数化的推理改进方法在无需额外训练的情况下提升了生成质量。
3. 吸收态重构方法在语言建模任务上匹配或超越了掩码扩散模型的性能。
4. 证明了掩码扩散与均匀扩散之间的经验差距主要源于参数化和采样设计，而非边际分布本身的选择。

Conclusion: 本文从理论层面深入分析了离散扩散模型中的参数化问题，提出了留一后验参数化和吸收态重构两种关键技术。研究结果表明，均匀扩散模型的性能瓶颈可以通过更好的参数化和采样设计来克服，为离散扩散模型的发展提供了重要的理论见解和实用方法。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [264] [Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22814)
*Lily Goli,Justin Kerr,Daniele Reda,Alec Jacobson,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合在线3D重建和序列模型的好奇心驱动强化学习方法，解决了复杂3D环境中智能体陷入局部循环的问题，实现了高效探索和零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的好奇心驱动强化学习在复杂、逼真的3D环境中容易陷入局部循环，原因是缺乏空间持久性和情节上下文，导致智能体在重新访问被遗忘状态时仍能获得新的奖励，从而阻碍有效探索。

Method: 采用在线3D重建作为世界的持久模型，并使用序列模型对RGB观测进行参数化以保持情节上下文。这种设计使智能体能够维护情节轨迹历史并导航到新颖区域，同时仅依赖RGB帧进行部署。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上仅通过好奇心训练，智能体性能优于基于RL的主动建图基线，并零样本泛化到Gibson和AI生成的世界。在下游任务（如苹果采摘和图像目标导航）中表现出色，优于从头训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: 有效的好奇心机制需要持久且持续更新的世界模型，以及能够保持情节轨迹历史的智能体，这对于稀疏奖励、长时域任务中的探索至关重要。

Abstract: Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.

</details>


### [265] [Integrable Elasticity via Neural Demand Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22820)
*Carlos Heredia,Daniel Roncel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ICDN模型，一种多产品零售需求神经模型，在Dominick's啤酒数据集上优于基准方法，产生更稳定且符合经济学原理的弹性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多产品零售需求预测需要准确估计价格弹性，但现有方法在交叉价格效应等弱识别场景下难以产生稳定、符合经济学原理的估计结果。

Method: 提出可积上下文相关需求网络(ICDN)，采用需求优先的神经网络架构，将对数需求学习为对数价格的平滑、上下文条件函数，从而能够从学习到的需求曲面精确推导弹性。

Result: 在Dominick's啤酒数据集上，ICDN在样本外泛化性能上优于有向对数-对数基准方法，并产生更稳定、更符合经济学原理的弹性估计，特别是在交叉价格效应等弱识别情况下表现更优。

Conclusion: ICDN模型成功实现了需求预测的准确性与经济学合理性的统一，为多产品零售场景下的需求分析和定价决策提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We propose the Integrable Context-Dependent Demand Network (ICDN), a demand-first neural model for multiproduct retail demand. The model learns log-demand as a smooth, context-conditioned function of log-prices, allowing elasticities to be derived exactly from the learned demand surface. On the Dominick's beer dataset, ICDN improves out-of-sample generalization over a directed log-log benchmark and yields more stable, economically plausible elasticity estimates, especially for weakly identified cross-price effects.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [DynaFlow: Transparent and Flexible Intra-Device Parallelism via Programmable Operator Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21603)
*Yi Pan,Yile Gu,Jinbin Luo,Yibo Wu,Ziren Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Ziyi Xu,Shengkai Lin,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出DynaFlow框架，通过解耦逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度，实现设备内并行策略的透明灵活集成，在6个SOTA ML系统中实现最高1.29倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML框架的静态顺序编程模型与设备内并行策略存在根本冲突，集成需要侵入式、模型特定的代码改造，工程成本过高；且策略对执行上下文敏感，需维护多个专门方案。

Method: DynaFlow框架：1) 灵活前端：支持图分区注释和可编程接口定义自定义设备内并行策略；2) 高效后端：异步管理复杂控制/数据流，自定义内存管理消除拷贝开销，保持与CUDA Graphs和TorchInductor等优化的兼容性；核心为解耦逻辑模型定义与物理执行调度。

Result: 在6个SOTA ML系统中成功集成代表性并行策略，仅需最小代码修改，实现最高1.29倍吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DynaFlow通过解耦设计实现了设备内并行策略的透明灵活集成，有效解决了资源利用率问题，同时保持了与现有优化技术的兼容性，为ML系统提供了低工程成本的并行优化方案。

Abstract: Intra-device parallelism addresses resource under-utilization in ML inference and training by overlapping the execution of operators with different resource usage. However, its wide adoption is hindered by a fundamental conflict with the static, sequential programming model of existing frameworks. Integrating these strategies requires invasive, model-specific code overhauls, representing an intractable engineering cost. This is further amplified by the high sensitivity of strategies to execution contexts (e.g., workload, model architecture, hardware), forcing developers to implement and maintain multiple specialized solutions. To address this, we propose DynaFlow, a framework that enables the transparent and flexible integration of intra-device parallelism by decoupling the logical model definition from the physical execution schedule. DynaFlow introduces a flexible frontend with annotations for graph partitioning and a programmable interface for defining custom intra-device parallelism strategies. Its efficient backend manages complex control/data-flow asynchronously, uses custom memory management to eliminate copy overheads, and preserves compatibility with optimizations like CUDA Graphs and TorchInductor. We demonstrate that DynaFlow can integrate representative parallelism strategies into 6 state-of-the-art ML systems with minimal code changes, achieving up to a 1.29x throughput improvement. DynaFlow is publicly available at https://github.com/uw-syfi/DynaFlow.

</details>


### [267] [LiveR: Fine-Grained Elasticity via Live Reconfiguration for Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22014)
*Haoyuan Liu,Kairui Zhou,Shuyao Qi,Qinwei Yang,Shengkai Lin,Shizhen Zhao,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LiveR是一种面向大模型训练的在线重配置运行时，通过避免停止-重启模式，将重配置停机时间从分钟级降至秒级，加速14-23倍，在波动资源条件下保持99%的训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为降低成本和提高集群利用率，大模型训练越来越多地使用现货实例等波动性廉价GPU资源。然而，现有弹性训练系统采用"停止-重启"方式进行重配置：通过检查点外化分布式状态、重建运行时并重启训练，导致每次调整都成为存储密集型恢复过程，产生大量停机时间（检查点I/O、进程重启、CUDA初始化、通信器设置）。

Method: LiveR提出了一种在线重配置运行时，用有限的内存传递替代存储支持的重新启动：1) 当前训练世界继续训练时，异步准备目标世界；2) 隔离启动新添加的工作节点，将重量级初始化移出关键路径；3) 通过高带宽互连直接流式传输模型状态，同时在线跨张量、流水线和数据并行维度进行重塑；4) 执行轻量级提交，无需在活动路径上停止-重启即可切换到新配置。基于Megatron-LM和PyTorch实现。

Result: 在多节点GPU集群上的端到端评估显示：LiveR将停机时间从分钟级降至秒级；相比检查点/重启基线加速重配置14-23倍；稳态开销极小；在波动资源条件下保持高达99%的训练吞吐量。

Conclusion: LiveR成功使波动性低成本GPU容量在大模型训练中变得实用可行，显著降低了资源成本的同时保证了训练效率，为大规模模型训练的经济性提供了有效的技术解决方案。

Abstract: To reduce user costs and maximize cluster utilization, large model training increasingly leverages volatile but inexpensive GPU capacity, such as spot instances and reclaimable resources in shared clusters. Yet, capitalizing on these economic benefits requires jobs to adapt within the short warning windows that many such environments provide. Existing elastic training systems still treat reconfiguration as stop-and-restart: they externalize distributed state through checkpoints, rebuild the distributed runtime on a new topology, and restart training, turning each resize event into a storage-heavy recovery procedure that incurs substantial downtime from checkpoint I/O, process restart, CUDA initialization, and communicator setup. We present LiveR, a live reconfiguration runtime for elastic LLM training that replaces storage-backed restart with a live, bounded-memory handoff between mixed-parallel training worlds. While the current world continues training, LiveR asynchronously prepares the target world, bootstraps newly added workers in isolation to keep heavyweight initialization off the critical path, and streams model state directly over high-bandwidth interconnects while reshaping it online across tensor, pipeline, and data parallel dimensions. Once the target world is ready, LiveR performs a lightweight commit that switches training to the new configuration without stop-and-restart on the live path. We implement LiveR atop Megatron-LM and PyTorch and evaluate it end-to-end on a multi-node GPU cluster. Across diverse reconfiguration scenarios, LiveR reduces downtime from minutes to seconds, accelerates reconfiguration by 14$\times$-23$\times$ over checkpoint/restart baselines, incurs minimal steady-state overhead, and sustains up to 99% training goodput under volatile-resource conditions, making volatile low-cost GPU capacity far more practical for LLM training.

</details>


### [268] [Secure and Parallel Determinant Computation for Large-Scale Matrices in Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22039)
*Prajwal Panth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPDC框架，通过轻量级加密方法CED（结合EWO和PRT）在分布式边缘服务器上实现隐私保护的并行矩阵行列式计算，并采用两种验证算法确保结果完整性，适用于资源受限的实时边缘计算场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算使资源受限的客户端能够将计算密集型任务委托给分布式边缘服务器，尤其在物联网环境中。矩阵行列式计算（MDC）在控制系统、密码学和机器学习应用中至关重要。然而，传统行列式算法的三次复杂度使其不适用于资源受限边缘场景中的实时处理。

Method: 提出安全并行行列式计算（SPDC）框架：1）使用复合元素失真（CED）轻量级加密方法，结合元素级混淆（EWO）和Panth旋转定理（PRT）来隐藏矩阵的结构和数值内容，同时保持行列式性质；2）采用并行LU分解将加密矩阵块分配到任意数量的不可信边缘服务器，实现高效可扩展的行列式计算；3）设计单向通信模型消除服务器间交互以减少协调开销；4）引入两种验证算法Q_2（概率标量方法）和Q_3（确定性低复杂度替代方案）以确保结果完整性。

Result: 数学分析表明，该框架提供了强隐私和安全保证、低计算开销和部署灵活性，使其非常适合分布式边缘辅助系统中的安全、可扩展和实时MDC应用。

Conclusion: SPDC框架成功解决了边缘计算环境中矩阵行列式计算的隐私保护、效率和可扩展性问题，为资源受限的物联网设备提供了一种安全可靠的计算外包方案，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: The advent of edge computing has enabled resource-constrained clients to delegate intensive computational tasks to distributed edge servers, especially within Internet of Things (IoT) environments. Among such tasks, Matrix Determinant Computation (MDC) remains critical for applications in control systems, cryptography, and machine learning. However, the cubic complexity of traditional determinant algorithms makes them unsuitable for real-time processing in constrained edge scenarios.
  We propose a Secure Parallel Determinant Computation (SPDC) framework, which provides strong security guaranties, including privacy-preserving MDC, across N distributed edge servers. The framework achieves privacy through Composite Element Distortion (CED) - a lightweight encryption method that combines Element-wise Obfuscation (EWO) and the Panth Rotation Theorem (PRT) to conceal both structural and numerical matrix content while preserving determinant properties. Parallel LU decomposition is used to distribute encrypted matrix blocks across an arbitrary number of untrusted edge servers, enabling efficient and scalable determinant computation. A one-way communication model further reduces coordination overhead by eliminating inter-server interactions. To ensure result integrity with minimal client burden, we further introduce two verification algorithms: Q_2, a probabilistic scalar method, and Q_3, a deterministic and low-complexity alternative.
  Mathematical analysis demonstrates that the proposed framework provides strong privacy and security guaranties, low computational overhead, and deployment flexibility - making it well-suited for secure, scalable, and real-time MDC in distributed edge-assisted systems.

</details>


### [269] [Nf-PEAK: Process-Based Energy Attribution for Nextflow Workflows on Kubernetes Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22393)
*Philipp Thamm,Somayeh Mohammadi,Kathleen West,Knut Reinert,Lauritz Thamsen,Ulf Leser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Nf-PEAK，一种容器化方法，用于在Kubernetes集群中将CPU和DRAM能耗精确归因到单个科学工作流任务，在并发负载下平均误差约10.9%，优于现有工具Kepler。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流任务异构性强，需要单独评估能耗以优化成本和可持续性；但在共享Kubernetes集群中，任务级能耗估计面临三大挑战：RAPL计数器仅报告节点级能耗、容器环境限制了对计数器和主机进程信息的访问、并发工作负载引入资源竞争和测量噪声。

Method: Nf-PEAK采用四步流程：(i)识别工作流Pod，(ii)通过cgroup元数据将Pod映射到主机进程，(iii)采样RAPL和每进程性能计数器，(iv)应用非线性能耗信用模型并在任务层级聚合结果。该方法是容器化的，专门针对Nextflow工作流引擎设计。

Result: 在Kubernetes集群上评估三个nf-core工作流：隔离运行时平均MAPE为6.6%；当无关工作负载饱和每节点32个硬件线程中的8个时，MAPE为10.9%；在2、3、4、8节点规模下保持稳定。与SOTA工具Kepler相比，Nf-PEAK平均误差更低，特别是在并发负载场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: Nf-PEAK成功解决了Kubernetes集群中科学工作流任务级能耗归因难题，为工作流能耗优化提供了可靠的基础，对成本控制和可持续发展具有重要意义。

Abstract: Scientific workflows are pipelines of interdependent tasks. They are increasingly executed on shared Kubernetes clusters via workflow engines such as Nextflow. Their energy consumption matters for both cost and sustainability. It is necessary to examine and optimize workflow tasks individually, because they can be very heterogeneous. However, estimating task-level energy on clusters is difficult: Intel RAPL counters report only node-level energy, access to counters and host process information is typically restricted, and concurrent workloads introduce resource contention and measurement noise. We present Nf-PEAK, a containerized method to attribute CPU-package and DRAM energy to individual processes and Nextflow tasks. Nf-PEAK (i) identifies workflow pods, (ii) maps pods to host processes via cgroup metadata, (iii) samples RAPL and per-process performance counters, and (iv) applies a non-linear energy-credit model before aggregating results at task level. On a Kubernetes cluster, we evaluate three nf-core workflows under controlled co-located CPU load. Nf-PEAK reaches an average Mean Absolute Percentage Error of 6.6% in isolated runs and 10.9% when an unrelated workload saturates 8 of 32 hardware threads per node, and remains stable across 2, 3, 4, and 8 nodes. Compared to the state-of-the-art Kubernetes tool Kepler, Nf-PEAK yields lower error on average, particularly under co-located load.

</details>


### [270] [Exploiting Multicast for Accelerating Collective Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22428)
*Chao Xu,Xu Zhang,Zihang Luo,Yuyan Wu,Guoxin Qian,Yufeng Yao,Chihyung Wang,Jingbin Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出MultiWrite语义，通过消除冗余数据包降低集体通信延迟，在Ascend NPU上实现最高33%延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练和推理中，AllGather和AlltoAll等集体通信是核心组件。现有实现基于单播写入，向多个接收者发送相同数据的重复副本，造成网络拥塞并增加端到端延迟。

Method: 提出MultiWrite多对多传输语义，采用多播原则消除冗余数据包传输，同时解决传统多播在AI工作负载中的管理平面开销过大和生态系统兼容性问题，在Ascend NPU上实现。

Result: 长期压力测试表明，基于MultiWrite实现的算子在商用设备上实现最高33%的延迟降低。

Conclusion: MultiWrite通过消除冗余数据包传输，有效降低了集体通信延迟，为大模型训练和推理提供了性能优化方案。

Abstract: Reducing collective communication latency is a critical goal for large model training and inference in both academia and industry. Many-to-many communications, such as AllGather and AlltoAll (dispatch), are core components of modern parallelization strategies. State-of-the-art implementations of these communications rely on unicast-based writes and transmit duplicate copies of the same data across physical links for multiple receivers. This redundant transmission congests network bottlenecks and degrades end-to-end latency. We present MultiWrite, a novel many-to-many transmission semantic that eliminates redundant packets to directly reduce operator latency. MultiWrite adopts multicast principles while addressing critical limitations of traditional multicast for AI workloads. These limitations include heavy management plane overhead and ecosystem compatibility issues. We implement MultiWrite on Ascend NPUs. Long-term stress tests demonstrate that our MultiWrite-based operators achieve up to 33% latency reduction on commercially deployed devices.

</details>


### [271] [Relay-Based Synchronization of Replicated Data Types in Opportunistic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22491)
*Frédéric Guidec,Yves Mahéo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在机会网络（OppNets）中使用移动中继节点来加速基于状态的CRDT副本收敛的方法，实验证明中继节点能显著改善收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机会网络中，信息传播仅依赖于移动设备之间短暂的配对无线接触，CRDT副本能否收敛以及收敛速度完全取决于设备间的接触机会。在这种具有挑战性的环境下，如何提高CRDT副本的收敛性能成为关键问题。

Method: 提出了一种利用移动中继节点来促进基于状态的CRDT副本在机会网络中收敛的方法。设计了新的协议以实现副本和中继节点之间的同步，并定义了新的度量指标来观察和刻画副本的收敛特性。通过仿真实验进行验证。

Result: 仿真结果表明，使用移动中继节点可以显著改善CRDT副本的收敛性能，甚至能够在仅靠副本自身无法收敛的场景下实现收敛。

Conclusion: 移动中继节点是提升机会网络中CRDT副本收敛性能的有效手段，能够克服机会网络中设备接触机会有限的限制，为在此类网络中部署分布式应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In Opportunistic Networks (OppNets), the dissemination of information can only rely on transient pairwise radio contacts between mobile devices (peers). Designing distributed applications that can run in such conditions is a challenge, but replicated data types, and in particular Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs), can help meet this challenge. A CRDT is inherently replicated data type whose replicas can be updated locally, yet eventually converge thanks to an anti-entropy algorithm that allows all replicas to synchronize in the background. Whether the replicas of a CRDT can actually converge in an OppNet, and how fast they can converge, depend on the occurrence of radio contacts between mobile devices. In this paper we investigate the idea of using mobile relays as a means to boost the convergence of stated-based CRDT replicas in an OppNet. New protocols are presented that allow the synchronization of replicas and relays, and new metrics are defined to observe and characterize the convergence of replicas. Simulation results show that using relays can significantly improve this convergence, and even make it possible in scenarios where the replicas alone would be unable to converge.

</details>


### [272] [AI-Driven Multi-Region Provisioning for Cloud Services Using Spot Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22778)
*Javier Fabra,Enrique Molina-Giménez,Pedro García-López*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个AI驱动的多云区域Spot实例集群配置服务，通过预测模型实现部署前的成本估算和跨区域优化，预测准确率达99.79%，成本节省高达64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务平台的Spot实例虽然提供折扣计算资源，但由于动态定价、资源可用性和中断风险的地域差异引入了不确定性。AWS EC2 Spot Service存在两个关键限制：无法在部署前估算集群成本，且配置仅限于单一区域，这阻碍了成本感知的跨区域部署决策。

Method: 提出了一个AI驱动的多云区域Spot集群配置服务，将配置计划监控与预测模型相结合。该方法能够在集群启动前估算配置方案和价格，支持跨区域的成本感知部署决策，同时保持与EC2 Spot Service的操作行为一致性。

Result: 在最大1500 vCPUs规模的集群上进行了验证。实验结果表明：预测准确率相比EC2 Spot Service达到99.79%；通过利用区域价格差异性，潜在成本节省可达64%。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了Spot实例配置中的成本预测和跨区域部署问题，为云服务平台提供了有效的成本优化解决方案，在保持操作一致性的同时实现了显著的成本节省。

Abstract: Cloud service platforms increasingly rely on elastic infrastructures to support dynamic workloads. Spot instances provide discounted computing resources but introduce uncertainty due to dynamic pricing, resource availability, and interruption risks that vary across geographical regions. In Amazon Web Services, the EC2 Spot Service simplifies fleet provisioning through allocation strategies, but it cannot estimate fleet costs before deployment and restricts provisioning to a single region. This paper presents an AI-driven provisioning service for multi-region spot fleets. The proposed approach combines monitoring of provisioning plans with predictive models to estimate fleet configurations and prices before launch, enabling cost-aware deployment decisions across regions while preserving the operational behavior of the EC2 Spot Service. The system was validated with fleets of up to 1500 vCPUs. Experimental results show a prediction accuracy of 99.79% compared to the EC2 Spot Service and potential cost savings of up to 64% by exploiting regional price variability.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [DeltaBox: Scaling Stateful AI Agents with Millisecond-Level Sandbox Checkpoint/Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22781)
*Yunpeng Dong,Jingkai He,Yuze Hou,Dong Du,Zhonghu Xu,Si Yu,Yubin Xia,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeltaBox系统，通过引入DeltaState这一新的OS级抽象，利用增量状态复制技术将AI代理沙箱的检查点和回滚延迟从百毫秒至秒级降低到毫秒级（检查点14ms，回滚5ms），显著提升了代理在固定时间预算下的状态探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的AI代理需要高频状态探索（如测试时树搜索和强化学习），这依赖于对完整沙箱状态（包括文件和进程状态）的快速检查点和回滚。现有机制通过全量复制实现，每次操作耗时数百毫秒至数秒，严重制约了深度搜索和大规模展开的性能。

Method: 提出DeltaState这一新OS级抽象，包含两个协同设计的机制：(1) DeltaFS：通过将文件状态组织为层结构，在检查点时动态冻结可写层并插入新层，将文件更新转化为写时复制，回滚简化为层切换；(2) DeltaCR：使用增量转储实现进程状态的变更式C/R，通过直接从冻结模板进程fork()来绕过传统流水线加速回滚。基于此实现了DeltaBox系统。

Result: 在SWE-bench和RL微基准测试上的评估显示，DeltaBox实现了毫秒级延迟（检查点14ms，回滚5ms），使AI代理在固定时间预算下能够探索更多节点。

Conclusion: 通过利用连续检查点间的高度相似性，采用增量复制而非全量复制的策略，配合OS级别的机制支持，可以实现对AI代理沙箱状态的毫秒级检查点和回滚，为高频状态探索场景提供了高效的系统支持。

Abstract: LLM-powered AI agents require high-frequency state exploration (e.g., test-time tree search and reinforcement learning), relying on rapid checkpoint and rollback (C/R) of the complete sandbox state, including files and process state (e.g., memory, contexts, etc.). Existing mechanisms duplicate the entire state, causing hundreds of milliseconds to seconds of latency per C/R, which severely bottlenecks deep search and large-scale fan-outs.
  This paper observes that subsequent checkpoints in AI agents are highly similar. Therefore, instead of full duplication, a sandbox should only duplicate the changes between consecutive checkpoints (Key Insight). However, it is non-trivial to realize the idea, mainly due to the missing OS supports. This paper proposes a new OS-level abstraction, DeltaState, to enable the change-based transactional C/R for AI agents with two co-designed OS mechanisms. First, DeltaFS enables change-based filesystem C/R by organizing the file states into layers and dynamically freezing the writable layer and inserting a new one during checkpoint, reducing file updates to copy-on-write, and making rollback a simple layer switch. Second, DeltaCR enables change-based process state C/R using incremental dumps, and accelerates rollback by bypassing traditional pipelines to directly fork() from a frozen template process. We then present DeltaBox, a novel agent sandbox achieving millisecond level C/R through the two new mechanisms. Evaluations on SWE-bench and RL micro-benchmarks show DeltaBox completes checkpoint and rollback in millisecond-level latency (14ms and 5ms, respectively), empowering agents to explore substantially more nodes under fixed time budgets.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [An Open-Source Framework to Emulate Delay and Disruption Tolerant Networks for International Space Station Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21624)
*Krit Grover,Marcelo Ponce*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源的、完整的束协议实现，用于与国际空间站通信，具备全面的安全特性，包括认证、完整性和加密机制，并提供交互式教育框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟/中断容忍网络（DTN）在挑战性网络环境（尤其是太空系统）中至关重要，因为端到端连通性无法保证。目前缺乏完整的、开源的束协议实现，作者旨在填补这一空白并提供教育资源。

Method: 实现了完整的束协议栈，包括束认证块（BAB）、载荷完整性块（PIB）和载荷机密性块（PCB），采用HMAC-SHA256和AES-256-CBC加密；实现了束分片与重组、优先级队列、托管传输（ACK/NAK机制）和自动重传；提供现代化响应式Web前端界面。

Result: 开发了一个开源、全栈的束协议实现，具备完整的安全特性，可用于与国际空间站通信，并提供交互式Web界面。

Conclusion: 该工作具有高度相关性：一方面展示了关键可靠协议的完整开源实现；另一方面为计算机网络与通信领域提供了交互式教育学习框架。

Abstract: Delay and Disruption Tolerant Networks (DTN) are critical for reliable communications in challenged network environments, particularly for space systems where end-to-end connectivity cannot be guaranteed. We present an open-source, full-stack implementation of the Bundle protocol for communicating with the International Space Station (ISS), with complete security features including Bundle Authentication Block (BAB), Payload Integrity Block (PIB), and Payload Confidentiality Block (PCB) using HMAC-SHA256 and AES-256-CBC encryption. The system includes bundle fragmentation and reassembly, priority-based queuing, custody transfer with ACK/NAK mechanisms, and automatic retransmission. Our system also includes a frontend facilitated by a modern responsive web interface. We consider this work highly relevant in the context of computer networking because: i) it demonstrates a full stack, open-source, freely available implementation of this critical and reliable protocol; and ii) it offers an interactive educational and learning framework in the field of computer networks and communications.

</details>


### [275] [Lost in the Prefix: Revisiting IP Geolocation Accuracy Across Networks and Geographies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21937)
*Syed Tauhidun Nabi,Jocelyn Bliton,Tijay Chung,Shaddi Hasan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 首次大规模评估四大IP地理定位数据库，发现移动网络误差比固定网络高10倍以上，全球南方地区失败率显著高于北方，根本原因是前缀粒度过粗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IP地理定位数据库在研究、政策和工业中被广泛使用，但其在不同网络类型和地理位置上的准确性尚未得到充分表征和系统性评估。

Method: 使用RIPE Atlas和UNICEF Giga提供的真实数据作为基准，对四个主要供应商（MaxMind GeoLite2、IPinfo、IP2Location和DB-IP）在175个国家进行大规模评估。

Result: 移动网络的中位误差是固定网络的10倍以上（179-207km vs 3-16km）；全球南方地区失败率显著更高（亚洲53-61%，非洲66-72%，欧洲仅9-20%）；移动网络和全球南方地区的前缀更粗糙，约70%的移动前缀地理跨度超过100km。

Conclusion: 前缀粒度是导致IP地理定位误差的共同解释因素：无论供应商、网络类型或地理位置如何，更粗糙的前缀始终产生最高误差，这揭示了当前IP地理定位数据库的结构性局限。

Abstract: IP geolocation databases are widely used in research, policy, and industry, yet their accuracy across network types and geographies remains poorly characterized. We present a large scale evaluation of four major providers (MaxMind GeoLite2, IPinfo, IP2Location, and DB-IP) using ground truth from RIPE Atlas and UNICEF Giga across 175 countries. We find that mobile networks exhibit median errors more than 10 times higher than fixed networks across all providers (179--207~km vs.\ 3--16~km), and that Global South regions show significantly higher failure rates than Global North: Asia exceeds 53--61\% and Africa 66--72\%, compared to 9--20\% in Europe. We trace both gaps to a shared structural source: provider prefixes in mobile networks and Global South geographies are more likely to be coarser than BGP announcements, and approximately 70\% of mobile prefixes span more than 100~km geographically. Our findings point to prefix granularity as a common explanatory factor: coarser prefixes consistently produce the highest errors regardless of provider, network type, or geography.

</details>


### [276] [Toward Realistic Wi-Fi Fault Diagnosis: A Multi-Modal Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22008)
*Junjian Zhang,Haobo Deng,Xinxin Li,Ming Zhao,Fengxiao Tang,Nei Kato*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个公开的多模态Wi-Fi故障数据集（超10,000个样本），并建立了统一基准测试，发现现有诊断方法在利用异构运维数据方面仍具挑战性，同时评估了LLM方法的诊断一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络中智能运维系统持续产生大量多模态运维数据，但异构运维环境下的Wi-Fi故障诊断研究仍不充分，缺乏公开的跨层运维观测数据集和统一评估基准。

Method: 1) 构建校园真实Wi-Fi测试平台并配备自动故障注入系统；2) 收集涵盖多种无线场景的多模态Wi-Fi故障数据集（10,000+样本）；3) 建立跨越多诊断任务、运维模态和代表性诊断范式的统一基准；4) 开发面向推理的评估框架以评估LLM生成诊断分析与实际网络状况的一致性。

Result: 1) 发布了首个公开捕获异构跨层运维观测的Wi-Fi故障诊断数据集；2) 实验表明现有诊断方法在有效利用异构运维数据方面仍存在挑战；3) LLM方法的评估揭示了生成诊断分析与实际状况的一致性问题，为未来多模态Wi-Fi诊断提供了重要参考。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了Wi-Fi故障诊断领域公开数据集的空白，通过统一基准测试揭示了现有方法的局限性，并指明了未来多模态Wi-Fi诊断的发展方向，强调了异构运维数据有效利用和LLM诊断一致性评估的重要性。

Abstract: Intelligent network operation and maintenance systems in modern networks continuously generate large volumes of multi-modal operational data. However, Wi-Fi fault diagnosis under heterogeneous operational environments remains insufficiently understood. We build a real-world Wi-Fi testbed deployed in campus working environments with an automated fault injection system, and collect a multi-modal Wi-Fi fault dataset containing over 10,000 fault samples across diverse wireless scenarios. To the best of our knowledge, this is among the first publicly available datasets jointly capturing heterogeneous cross-layer operational observations for Wi-Fi fault diagnosis. Based on this dataset, we establish a unified benchmark spanning multiple diagnosis tasks, operational modalities, and representative diagnosis paradigms. Experimental results indicate that effectively leveraging heterogeneous operational data remains challenging for existing diagnosis approaches. We further evaluate emerging LLM-based approaches and develop a reasoningoriented evaluation framework to assess the consistency between generated diagnostic analyses and actual network conditions. Our findings suggest several important considerations for future multi-modal Wi-Fi diagnosis.

</details>


### [277] [Astragalus: Automatic Configuration Repair for Production Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22092)
*Zhenrong Gu,Peng Zhang,Xing Feng,Xu Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Astragalus，一种基于语法驱动的网络配置自动修复方法，避免了传统语义驱动方法中昂贵的SMT约束求解问题，通过"定位-修复-验证"迭代管道实现高效修复，在合成网络和真实生产网络中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的网络配置自动修复（ACR）工具采用语义驱动方法，将网络语义建模为SMT约束集合并求解，但由于网络语义复杂，构建和求解这些约束成本过高，导致现有工具缺乏通用性和可扩展性。因此需要一种更高效的修复方法。

Method: 受自动程序修复（APR）启发，提出了语法驱动方法Astragalus。该方法不建模程序语义，而是通过从同一代码库中"嫁接"现有代码来修复bug。采用多次迭代的"定位-修复-验证"管道来搜索修复方案。

Result: 在注入15种类型错误的测试中：能够在多种规模的合成网络中修复所有事故；在真实网络中修复97.5%的事故；平均修复时间为7.36秒。在包含O(1,000)Õ(10,000)设备的真实生产网络中，对4个近期的网络事故或非预期变更，在6分钟内提供了有效的修复方案。

Conclusion: 语法驱动方法为网络配置自动修复提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案，Astragalus在实际生产网络中表现出良好的实用性和效率，能够快速处理大规模网络中的配置错误。

Abstract: Network configurations are prone to errors, which can lead to catastrophic service outages. A tool that can achieve automatic configuration repair (ACR) is highly desired by operators. Existing tools for ACR follow a semantic-driven approach: they model network semantics as a set of SMT constraints, and solve them for a location or fix of the error. Due to the complex semantics of networks, constructing and solving these constraints can be prohibitively expensive, making these tools neither general nor scalable. Inspired by automatic program repair (APR), we explore another direction, i.e., a syntax-driven approach, which tries to repair program bugs by ``grafting'' some existing code in the same repository, without modeling program semantics. Following this direction, we propose Astragalus, a syntax-driven method for ACR. It uses multiple iterations of a ``localize-fix-validate'' pipeline to search for repairs, and proves quite effective on configurations of our production network. Specifically, we show that Astragalus can repair every incident in multiple sizes of a synthesized network, and 97.5\% of the incidents on a real network, both with 15 types of errors injected, within an average time of 7.36 seconds. It has also provided valid repair options in under 6 minutes for 4 recent network incidents or undesired changes, in a real production network with O(1,000)Õ(10,000) devices.

</details>


### [278] [High-speed Networking for Giga-Scale AI Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21187)
*Sajy Khashab,Albert Gran Alcoz,Alon Gal,Jacky Romano,Rani Abboud,Yonatan Piasetzky,Lior Maman,Amit Nishry,Barak Gafni,Omer Shabtai,Matty Kadosh,Dror Goldenberg,Gilad Shainer,Mark Silberstein*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NVIDIA Spectrum-X通过多平面架构和硬件加速负载均衡，实现了大规模AI训练网络的高性能、低延迟和高可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式模型训练扩展到数十万GPU规模，横向扩展网络面临前所未有的性能和效率需求，需要实现可预测且稳定的网络性能、高利用率和低延迟。

Method: 提出Spectrum-X多平面架构，用拓扑并行性替代层次化深度，并在网卡和交换机中引入硬件加速负载均衡，以微秒级时间尺度快速响应动态网络条件。

Result: 评估表明：达到98%理论线速率且低抖动延迟；强跨租户隔离；鲁棒的双分带宽，10%链路故障仅增加7%延迟；在LLM训练中快速响应链路翻动。

Conclusion: Spectrum-X网络架构成功满足了大规模AI基础设施的性能需求，在实际部署和调试中验证了其在生产环境中的有效性和可靠性。

Abstract: As distributed model training scales to span hundreds of thousands of GPUs, scale-out networks face unprecedented performance and efficiency demands. NVIDIA Spectrum-X Ethernet has been designed from the ground up to achieve predictable and stable network performance with high utilization and low latency. This paper presents the Spectrum-X multiplane architecture, which replaces hierarchical depth with topological parallelism, and introduces hardware-accelerated load balancing in NICs and switches as the key architectural approach to provide fast reaction to highly dynamic network conditions at the microsecond timescales that AI training workloads demand. We describe the motivation, design principles, evaluation methodology and performance on state-of-the-art benchmarks, as well as the lessons we learned from deploying and debugging Spectrum-X networks in large-scale systems. Our evaluation highlights production-grade AI infrastructure performance across three core dimensions: 98% of the theoretical line rate with low jitter-free latency; strong cross-tenant isolation for concurrent workloads; robust, capacity-proportional bisection bandwidth and 7% latency increase for 10% fabric link failures; and rapid reaction to host and fabric link flaps during LLM training workloads.

</details>


### [279] [Latency in Real-Time 3D Volumetric Streaming: A Comprehensive Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22131)
*Seungwoo Hong,Hosun Yoon,Seong Moon,Inayat Ali*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对实时3D体积流媒体进行了全面的延迟测量与分析，通过分层方法识别瓶颈并提出优化策略，以提升系统响应性和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时3D体积流媒体在VR、AR、游戏、远程协作等领域具有重要应用价值，但延迟问题严重影响用户体验（包括沉浸感降低、晕动症、实时交互中断），亟需系统性研究以解决延迟瓶颈。

Method: 采用分层分析方法，将流媒体过程系统分解为三个关键层：应用层、传输协议层和网络层，在真实系统中对每层进行延迟评估，识别瓶颈并量化其影响。

Result: 成功识别了实时体积流媒体环境中的延迟瓶颈，量化了各层的延迟影响，揭示了延迟的根本原因，并提出了针对性的优化策略以减轻延迟并增强系统响应性。

Conclusion: 本研究为实时3D体积流媒体建立了最佳实践和创新解决方案，在效率、可扩展性和用户体验方面取得显著改进，为构建更沉浸、更响应的数字环境奠定了基础。

Abstract: Real-time 3D volumetric streaming is a transformative technology that enables the seamless transmission and rendering of high-fidelity 3D models, enhancing applications in virtual reality (VR), augmented reality (AR), gaming, telepresence, and remote collaboration. However, latency remains a major challenge, affecting immersion, causing motion sickness, and disrupting real-time interactions. Addressing these latency issues is essential for improving user experience and ensuring system efficiency. This study conducts a comprehensive latency measurement and analysis within a real-time volumetric streaming environment. We systematically break down the streaming process into three key layers: the application layer, the transport protocol layer, and the network layer. By evaluating each layer in a real-world system, we identify latency bottlenecks, quantify their impact, and uncover the underlying causes of delay. Based on these findings, we propose targeted optimization strategies to mitigate latency and enhance system responsiveness. Through this research, we establish best practices and innovative solutions to improve the efficiency, scalability, and overall user experience of real-time 3D volumetric streaming. Our insights contribute to advancing the field, paving the way for more immersive and responsive digital environments.

</details>


### [280] [Impact of Atmospheric Turbulence and Pointing Error on Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22268)
*Celia Sánchez-de-Miguel,Antonio M. Mercado-Martínez,Beatriz Soret,Antonio Jurado-Navas,Miguel Castillo-Vázquez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大气湍流和指向抖动对地球观测图像中船只检测性能的影响，发现YOLOv8在退化条件下性能显著下降，而RetinaNet表现更为稳健，强调了在训练数据中包含真实物理退化的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测图像常受大气湍流和指向抖动影响而退化，但现有AI检测模型的训练数据集很少考虑这些因素，导致模型在实际运行环境中可能出现性能下降。

Method: 提出增强型图像模拟器，整合垂直路径大气湍流和由平台与传感器振动引起的卫星指向抖动，生成物理真实的退化图像；使用YOLOv8和RetinaNet在不同湍流和指向误差水平下进行船只检测作为案例研究。

Result: YOLOv8的召回率从理想条件下的91%下降到弱湍流条件下的60%，在强湍流或抖动条件下降至40%以下；RetinaNet表现更稳健，在退化条件下保持约75%的召回率。

Conclusion: 在地球观测训练数据集中纳入真实的物理退化对于确保AI模型在运行环境中的可靠性能至关重要，特别是在海上监视应用中。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) imagery is often degraded by atmospheric turbulence and pointing jitter; yet, these effects are rarely considered in datasets used to train AI-based detection models. Based on prior work, this paper presents an enhanced image simulator that enables the incorporation of vertical-path atmospheric turbulence and satellite pointing jitter, arising from platform and sensor vibrations, to generate physically realistic distorted images. As a case study, vessel detection is evaluated using YOLOv8 and RetinaNet on images generated by the proposed simulator under different levels of turbulence and pointing errors. Results show that YOLOv8 recall decreases from 91% under ideal conditions to 60% in the presence of weak turbulence, and falls below 40% under strong turbulence or jitter. In contrast, RetinaNet demonstrates greater robustness, maintaining approximately 75% recall across degraded conditions. These results highlight the importance of incorporating realistic physical degradations into EO training datasets to ensure reliable performance of AI-based models in operational environments, as demonstrated in maritime surveillance applications.

</details>


### [281] [Throughput and Delay Performance of Slotted Aloha in SmartBANs under Saturation Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22317)
*Anastasios C. Politis,Constantinos S. Hilas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文利用二维离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）对ETSI SmartBAN标准下的时隙Aloha协议进行了建模与饱和性能分析，理论模型与仿真结果高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估ETSI SmartBAN规范定义的时隙Aloha协议在饱和条件下的性能表现，为协议的性能预测和优化提供理论依据。

Method: 构建二维离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）模型，详细刻画协议的操作细节，并在饱和条件下分析饱和吞吐量和平均端到端延迟。

Result: 通过仿真验证了所提模型的准确性，理论分析与仿真结果之间表现出高度一致性；模型能够有效预测协议性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的DTMC模型能够准确描述SmartBAN时隙Aloha协议的行为，可作为协议性能预测与优化的有效工具。

Abstract: This letter evaluates the performance of the slotted Aloha protocol defined by the European Telecommunication Standard Institute (ETSI) SmartBAN specification, under saturation conditions. For this purpose, we develop a two-dimensional Discrete Time Markov Chain (DTMC) to model the operational details of the protocol and assess its performance in terms of saturation throughput and average end-to-end delay. The accuracy of the proposed model is validated by means of simulation which reveals a very good match among theoretical and simulation results. The model can be used for protocol performance prediction and optimization purposes.

</details>


### [282] [Eliminating Premature Termination in Multihop Rendezvous for Cognitive Radio-based Emergency Response Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22325)
*Zahid Ali,Saritha Unnikrishnan,Eoghan Furey,Ian McLoughlin,Saim Ghafoor*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MR-DMCA算法，解决了多跳会合协议中的过早终止问题，在应急通信场景中实现了100%的邻居和拓扑发现准确率，同时将会合时间降低了最多76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后环境中通信基础设施受损严重，而现有认知无线电网络的多跳会合协议存在N-1终止条件导致的过早终止问题，这会导致邻居发现不完整和网络拓扑形成无效，这一问题此前被忽视。

Method: 提出多跳可靠双模时钟算法(MR-DMCA)，引入坐标辅助的邻居验证机制和自主终止策略，确保在协议终止前完成完整的邻居和拓扑发现。该验证和终止方法适用于更广泛的多跳会合协议类别。

Result: 在20节点、20信道、高主用户活动(m=2)的最坏可扩展场景下，MR-DMCA实现了100%准确的邻居和拓扑发现，相比基准协议分别降低了76%、37%和17%的会合时间。

Conclusion: 解决过早终止问题对于基于认知无线电的应急通信网络中可靠的多跳会合至关重要，所提出的验证和终止机制能有效确保网络拓扑的可靠形成。

Abstract: In post-disaster environments, damaged communication infrastructure severely limits coordination among emergency response teams. Cognitive radio networks (CRNs) enable rapidly deployable communication by allowing nodes to opportunistically access available spectrum. However, existing multihop rendezvous protocols typically rely on N-1 termination conditions, which can lead to premature termination, resulting in incomplete neighbour discovery and invalid network topology formation. This work identifies this limitation as a previously overlooked issue in multihop rendezvous protocols. This paper proposes a Multihop Reliable Dual-Modular Clock Algorithm (MR-DMCA) that eliminates premature termination and ensures reliable network formation. The proposed protocol introduces a coordinate-assisted neighbour validation mechanism and an autonomous termination strategy that guarantees complete neighbour and topology discovery before protocol termination. Although implemented within MR-DMCA, the proposed validation and termination approach is applicable to a wider class of multihop rendezvous protocols. Extensive simulations demonstrate that, in a worst-case scalable scenario with 20 nodes and 20 channels under high primary radio activity (m=2), MR-DMCA achieves 100% accurate neighbour and topology discovery while reducing rendezvous time by up to 76%, 37%, and 17% compared with baseline protocols. The results highlight that addressing premature termination is critical for reliable multihop rendezvous in cognitive radiobased emergency communication networks.

</details>


### [283] [EnCoR: An end-to-end architecture for simplifying cellular networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22524)
*Wesley Woo,Zhuowei Wen,Monniiesh Velmurugan,Richard Raad,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Scott Shenker,Shaddi Hasan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出EnCoR架构，通过移除蜂窝网络核心网中的移动性支持，改用端到端移动性方案，在降低延迟、减少成本（超过90%）和提升切换性能的同时，保持与传统LTE网络的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蜂窝网络在网络内部提供移动性支持虽然为传统流量提供无缝连接，但会带来终端用户延迟增加、复杂度提升和运营开销增加等副作用。而现代应用和传输协议对移动性的容忍度不断提高，促使研究者重新审视移动性必须作为网内服务提供的假设。

Method: 提出EnCoR（End-to-End Core and RAN）架构，完全从核心网中移除移动性支持，采用端到端移动性方案，消除基于隧道的IP锚定，同时保持与现有认证、计费和QoS技术的兼容性。该架构可与未修改的手机终端协同工作。

Result: EnCoR在实际应用（视频、语音通话、视频流）中提供与传统LTE网络相当的性能；能够降低端到端延迟；根据FCC的成本估算，提供低延迟服务的资本成本相比3GPP网络降低超过90%；在相同硬件约束下核心网可处理更多切换事件，负载下切换延迟降低2.6倍。

Conclusion: EnCoR证明了将移动性支持从核心网移除并采用端到端方案是可行的，能够在保持系统兼容性和应用性能的同时，显著降低延迟、基础设施成本和切换控制开销，为未来蜂窝网络架构设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Since their creation, cellular networks have made in-network mobility support a key feature of their service model. While this approach provides seamless connectivity for legacy traffic, it has the side effects of inflating end-user latency and increasing complexity and operational overhead for operators. Yet modern applications and transport protocols are increasingly mobility tolerant, prompting us to revisit the assumption that mobility must be provided as an in-network service. In this paper, we propose EnCoR (End-to-End Core and RAN), a deployable cellular network architecture that removes mobility from the core entirely. Leveraging end-to-end mobility, EnCoR eliminates tunnel-based IP anchoring while preserving compatibility with existing authentication, charging, and QoS techniques. We demonstrate that EnCoR works with unmodified phones while providing equivalent performance as traditional LTE networks for real applications including video and voice calling and video streaming. We show that EnCoR not only allows network operators to reduce end to end latency, but can also reduce the capital cost of providing low latency service to users by more than 90% compared to 3GPP networks, based on cost estimates for cellular network core and border router infrastructure provided by the FCC. Finally, we demonstrate that these gains are achieved while reducing the amount of overall handover control messaging, allowing the EnCoR core network to handle a greater number of mobility handover events than an LTE core under identical hardware constraints, achieving a 2.6x lower handover latency under load.

</details>


### [284] [SCALE: Sensitivity-Aware Federated Unlearning with Information Freshness Optimization for Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22589)
*Zihao Ding,Beining Wu,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCALE框架，一种双层遗忘方法，通过历史贡献分析和信息新鲜度感知的自适应稀疏化，解决了现有联邦遗忘方法在遗忘精度低和时间信息缺失方面的问题，在移动边缘计算系统中实现了更优的数据遗忘效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦遗忘方法存在遗忘精度低且缺乏时间信息反映的问题，导致遗忘性能欠佳，无法有效应对数据污染和满足严格的隐私法规要求。

Method: 提出SCALE双层遗忘框架：首先基于历史贡献的层敏感性分析识别受目标客户端影响最大的层；然后在权重子组层面通过自适应稀疏化执行细粒度遗忘，以平衡信息新鲜度与遗忘有效性；并进行理论分析证明收敛性和加速优势。

Result: 实验和测试平台结果表明，该方法在遗忘效果方面显著优于最先进的基线方法，遗忘性能得到显著提升。

Conclusion: SCALE框架成功解决了联邦遗忘中的关键挑战，为移动边缘计算系统中的隐私保护和数据管理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Unlearning (FU) is emerging as a powerful tool that enables the selective removal of client data to effectively address data contamination and meet strict privacy regulations in mobile edge computing (MEC) systems. Although FU has recently drawn attention in the AI community, existing approaches suffer from low unlearning precision and lack temporal information reflection, which results in suboptimal forgetting performance. To address these issues, we propose SCALE, a dual-level unlearning framework combining historical contribution analysis with information freshness-aware adaptive sparsification. Our framework first employs a historical contribution-based layer sensitivity analysis to identify layers most influenced by target clients, then performs fine-grained unlearning through adaptive sparsification at the weight sub-group level to balance information freshness with forgetting effectiveness. Through theoretical analysis, the proposed framework demonstrates the convergence properties and acceleration advantages. Our experiments and testbed results demonstrate superior unlearning effectiveness compared to state-of-the-art baselines, with significantly improved forgetting performance.

</details>


### [285] [An intensive vRAN deployment with OpenAirInterface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22638)
*Romain Beurdouche,Raymond Knopp*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在通用处理器和硬件加速器上部署5G虚拟化无线接入网（vRAN）的实践经验，通过优化OpenAirInterface软件栈实现了多实例共享服务器的规模化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G虚拟化无线接入网（vRAN）的出现对计算机架构提出了新挑战：需要选择或设计能够提供足够性能，同时最大化网络灵活性和效率的计算技术。

Method: 基于OpenAirInterface 5G软件栈，在通用处理器和硬件加速器架构上进行vRAN密集部署；适配软件栈以利用硬件能力，实现多个vRAN实例共享服务器的扩展方案。

Result: 成功实现了vRAN密集部署，展示了软件栈改进及其对性能的影响，观察并分析了不同计算机架构的行为特征及其对部署的影响。

Conclusion: 总结了当前部署方案的局限性，并提出了进一步改进vRAN部署的方向和措施。

Abstract: The advent of 5G virtualized Radio Access Networks (vRANs) brings a new challenge with regards to computer architectures. It requires to select or design computing technologies that provide a sufficient level of performance while maximizing the flexibility and efficiency of the implemented networks. Several solutions addressing this challenge were proposed, relying on general purpose processors as well as hardware accelerators. This work describes our effort to enable an intensive vRAN deployment using the 5G software stack OpenAirInterface on top of these computer architectures. We had to adapt the software stack to leverage the capabilities of hardware and to find how to scale up the vRAN deployment with several vRAN instances sharing a server. We describe in this work our improvements to the stack and their effect on performance. We also share our observations on the behavior of the computer architectures and how they affect our deployment. We finally discuss the limitations of our deployment and further efforts to implement better vRAN deployments.

</details>
